### [28] [ACE: Self-Evolving LLM Coding Framework via Adversarial Unit Test Generation and Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16299)
*Yixu Huang,Xinglei Yu,Zhongyu Wei*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ACE 是一个基于求解器-对抗器架构的代码生成框架，通过执行结果监督（非地面真实代码）实现自我进化，在多个代码生成基准测试中取得3-7%的pass@1绝对提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代码生成依赖大规模标注和验证监督，但验证器在求解器变强后失效（生成的测试无法暴露失败模式），需要一种不依赖外部奖励模型或标注数据的自进化方法。

Method: 采用求解器-对抗器架构：同一LLM交替生成候选程序和对立的单元测试输入（诱导运行时错误、异常等执行失败），通过Kahneman-Tversky优化从执行结果中学习，全程无需真实代码或外部奖励模型。

Result: 在CodeContests、MBPP、LiveCodeBench上，ACE比强求解器-验证器基线高出3-7% pass@1，在分布外基准上提升更大，且推理效率具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 执行优先的对抗式自我进化是突破代码生成缩放瓶颈的有效范式，无需外部监督信号即可实现持续自我提升。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) excel at code generation but remain heavily reliant on large-scale annotated solutions and verification-based supervision, which constrains scalability and hinders sustained self-improvement. Recent solver--verifier frameworks exploit program execution as an automatic supervision signal, but their effectiveness degrades as solvers become moderately strong: verifier-generated tests increasingly confirm semantic correctness rather than exposing the remaining failure modes. We propose \textbf{ACE}, a self-evolving code generation framework based on a solver--adversary architecture that prioritizes active failure discovery through execution-centric supervision. A single LLM alternates between generating candidate programs and producing adversarial unit test inputs optimized to induce execution-level failures, such as runtime errors, exceptions, or non-termination. Supervision is derived solely from execution outcomes: robust programs are selected for supervised fine-tuning, while adversarial tests are optimized via Kahneman--Tversky Optimization using execution-derived preferences. Notably, the entire training loop requires no ground-truth code or external reward models. Experiments on CodeContests, MBPP, and LiveCodeBench demonstrate that ACE consistently outperforms strong solver--verifier baselines, achieving 3--7\% absolute gains in pass@1, with larger improvements on out-of-distribution benchmarks, while maintaining competitive or improved inference efficiency.

</details>


### [29] [Customizing an LLM for Enterprise Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16517)
*Aditya Kini,Satish Chandra,Milad Hashemi,Saksham Thakur,Aditya Pandey,Vincent Nguyen,Marc Brockschmidt,Franjo Ivančić,Danny Tarlow,Parthasarathy Ranganathan,Petros Maniatis,Ahmed Omran,Zaheer Abbas,Anita Gergely,Martin Sevenich,Gufeng Zhang,Amy Hua,Alexander Frömmgen Ranganathan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了谷歌为内部软件工程生态定制的LLM——Gemini for Google (GfG)，从万亿tokens专有数据集的构建到中训练策略的实施，并在29000名开发者的大规模盲测中显著降低了迭代次数（23%）并提高了代码存活率（17%），提供了企业模型自适应的完整蓝图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业软件开发产生的丰富数据可用于微调LLM以解锁更多工具能力，尽管前沿LLM已很强大，但针对企业特定生态的定制化优化仍有巨大价值。

Method: 从谷歌内部软件工程数据中构建万亿tokens专有数据集，采用中训练（mid-training）策略缓解灾难性遗忘，进行全栈模型调优（包括持续预训练和后训练），并在29000名开发者中进行大规模盲测A/B实验。

Result: 在盲测中，Gemini for Google相比基线将每轮平均迭代次数减少23%，代码存活率提高约17%。

Conclusion: 该方法为企业利用内部工程数据定制LLM提供了可复制的蓝图，涵盖信号提取、数据准备、模型调优和下游应用部署。

Abstract: Enterprise software development is a continuous evolutionary process, characterized by incremental additions, architectural revisions, production deployments and rigorous maintenance. These activities generate valuable data that modern LLMs could be finetuned on, to unlock additional tool possibilities for enterprise software engineering. While frontier LLMs are already very capable, this form of customization offers a compelling path for enterprise-specific optimization.
  We introduce Gemini for Google (GfG)}, an adaptation of Gemini specialized for Google's internal software engineering ecosystem. This paper details the model's end-to-end development, from curating a trillion-token proprietary dataset to implementing a mid-training strategy that mitigates catastrophic forgetting. In a large-scale blind A/B study across 29,000 developers, Gemini for Google significantly outperformed baselines: reducing the mean number of iterations per turn by 23\%, and increasing code survival rates by about 17%. Beyond metrics, we provide a comprehensive blueprint for enterprise model adaptation, covering: (1)The extraction of high-value signals from software engineering data, (2)Data preparation strategies, (3)Full-stack model tuning (continued pre-training and post-training), and (4)The deployment of downstream applications. We believe this methodology offers a replicable path for other organizations to unlock the full potential of their internal engineering data.

</details>


### [30] [Beyond Execution: Static-Analysis Rewards and Hint-Conditioned Diffusion RL for Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17174)
*Shuyin Ouyang,Zhaozhi Qian,Faroq AL-Tam,Muhammad AL-Qurishi,Jie M. Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了扩散语言模型在代码生成中的强化学习后训练，发现静态检查是最优的免执行奖励，且奖励设计和提示条件采样对任务难度敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型在复杂代码生成任务中存在"能力悬崖"问题，即基于执行的语义奖励过低导致学习信号不足，亟需探索更有效的奖励设计和训练策略。

Method: 沿三个维度（奖励设计、提示条件采样、任务难度）进行系统实证研究，比较免执行奖励（静态检查、AST提示等）与传统单元测试执行奖励，并在HumanEval、MBPP、LiveCodeBench上评估。

Result: 静态检查是整体最优的免执行奖励，将DiffuCoder在HumanEval上从53.9提升至67.1，LiveCodeBench从14.9提升至15.5，同时减少9.4%的rollout时间；AST提示对困难任务最有效；基于相似度的奖励在简单子集上更有效，静态检查在困难子集上更可靠。

Conclusion: 奖励设计和训练指导对扩散RL在代码生成中的性能有实质性影响，不同难度任务需要差异化策略。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) is an important paradigm for aligning Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) toward functional correctness in code generation. However, these models often encounter a ``capability cliff'' on complex tasks, where execution-based semantic rewards become too low to provide a viable learning signal. In this paper, we present a systematic empirical study of RL post-training for diffusion-based code generation along three axes: reward design, hint-conditioned sampling, and task difficulty. We investigate the effectiveness of execution-free rewards as alternatives to traditional unit-test execution, the role of training-time hint-conditioned diffusion sampling in mitigating exploration bottlenecks, and the impact of these design choices varies across tasks with different difficulty levels. Across HumanEval, MBPP, and LiveCodeBench, we find that static checking is the strongest overall standalone execution-free reward in our setting, especially improving DiffuCoder from 53.9 to 67.1 on HumanEval and from 14.9 to 15.5 on LiveCodeBench while reducing rollout time by 9.4\%. We further find that moderate AST-based hinting is most useful on harder benchmarks, while the best reward design depends strongly on task difficulty: similarity-based rewards are more effective on easier subsets, whereas static checking is more reliable on harder subsets where execution rewards are low. These findings suggest that reward design and training guidance substantially affect diffusion RL performance in our evaluated code-generation setting.

</details>


### [31] [Debug Like a Human: Scaling LLM-based Fault Localization to Processor Design via Block-Level Instruction-Oriented Slicing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17290)
*Zizhen Liu,Xiaoguang Mao,Deheng Yang,Jiayu He,Yihao Qin,Guangda Zhang,Yan Lei,Jianjun Xu,Jiang Wu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出BluesFL，一种基于LLM的块级故障定位框架，通过数据流代码分块和指令切片算法，在19K行RISC-V处理器设计中实现24个bug的Top-1定位，相比现有最优方法提升242.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代处理器设计验证中，故障定位是关键且耗时的步骤。现有LLM技术在模块级硬件设计上表现良好，但面对大规模项目级处理器设计时，自动定位bug仍具挑战。

Method: 提出基于数据流的代码块化方法引导LLM关注关键局部上下文，以及块级指令导向切片算法（Blues）使LLM模拟人类工程师分析指令执行路径和处理器状态的推理过程。

Result: 在真实RISC-V处理器核心（19K行SystemVerilog代码）上，Top-1正确定位24个bug，较现有最优方法（7个）提升242.9%，平均每个bug定位成本仅0.257美元。

Conclusion: BluesFL在大规模处理器设计中显著优于现有方法，成本可控，证明了LLM在复杂硬件故障定位中的潜力。

Abstract: Fault localization in modern processor design code is a critical yet time-consuming step during processor verification. While recent advances in LLM-based techniques for module-level hardware design have shown promising results, automatically localizing bugs in large-scale, project-level processor designs remains challenging. In this paper, we present BluesFL, a novel block-level LLM-based fault localization framework for processor designs. Inspired by the way engineers debug processors, we first propose a dataflow-based code blockization approach to guide LLMs to focus on critical local code context. We further propose a Block-Level Instruction-Oriented Slicing (Blues) algorithm that enables LLMs to mimic human reasoning by analyzing instruction execution paths and processor states. We evaluate BluesFL on a real-world RISC-V processor core comprising 19K lines of SystemVerilog code. Experimental results demonstrate that BluesFL correctly localizes 24 bugs at Top-1, achieving 242.9% improvement over the existing state-of-the-art (7 bugs). Cost analysis shows that BluesFL requires an average of only $0.257 to localize a single bug.

</details>


### [32] [SaaSBench: Exploring the Boundaries of Coding Agents in Long-Horizon Enterprise SaaS Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17526)
*Qingnan Ren,Shun Zou,Shiting Huang,Ziao Zhang,Kou Shi,Zhen Fang,Yiming Zhao,Yu Zeng,Qisheng Su,Lin Chen,Yong Wang,Zehui Chen,Xiangxiang Chu,Feng Zhao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SaaSBench 是一个专为企业级 SaaS 系统设计的 AI 智能体基准测试，涵盖 30 个复杂任务、6 个 SaaS 领域、8 种编程语言、6 种数据库和 13 种框架，揭示了当前最先进智能体的主要瓶颈不在生成代码逻辑，而在于配置和集成多组件系统（超过 95% 的失败发生在触及深层业务逻辑之前）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试仅局限于结构简化的单栈应用，无法捕捉真实企业 SaaS 系统中的异构环境、全栈编排和系统级复杂性，缺乏在现实工程约束下评估智能体的标准。

Method: 构建了包含 30 个跨 6 个 SaaS 域的复杂任务基准，集成 8 种编程语言、6 种数据库和 13 种框架；设计了面向长周期、多组件耦合系统的依赖感知混合评估范式。

Result: 超过 95% 的任务失败发生在到达深层业务逻辑之前，主要原因是模型在基础系统搭建阶段因过度自信而提前终止或陷入无效调试循环。

Conclusion: 当前 AI 编码智能体的主要瓶颈在于配置和集成多组件系统而非生成孤立代码，SaaSBench 可作为推动可靠系统级编码智能体演进的实用且具挑战性的测试平台。

Abstract: As autonomous coding agents become capable of handling increasingly long-horizon tasks, they have gradually demonstrated the potential to complete end-to-end software development. Although existing benchmarks have recently evolved from localized code editing to from-scratch project generation, they remain confined to structurally simplified, single-stack applications. Consequently, they fail to capture the heterogeneous environments, full-stack orchestration, and system-level complexity of real enterprise Software as a Service (SaaS) systems, leaving a critical gap in assessing agents under realistic engineering constraints. To fill this gap, we introduce SaaSBench, the first benchmark designed to explore the boundaries of AI agents in enterprise SaaS engineering. Spanning 30 complex tasks across 6 SaaS domains with 5,370 validation nodes, it incorporates 8 programming languages, 6 databases, and 13 frameworks to meticulously mirror real-world software heterogeneity. Furthermore, we design a dependency-aware hybrid evaluation paradigm tailored for complex systems with long horizons and multi-component coupling, enabling fine-grained, reproducible assessment. Crucially, our extensive experiments reveal a striking insight: the primary bottleneck for state-of-the-art agents is not generating isolated code logic, but successfully configuring and integrating a multi-component system. Over 95\% of task failures occur before agents even reach deep business logic, with models often falling victim to overconfidence and prematurely halting during foundational system setup, or getting trapped in ineffective debugging loops. We hope SaaSBench serves as a practical and challenging testbed to drive the evolution of reliable, system-level coding agents. The code is available at \url{https://github.com/ShadeCloak/SaaSbench}.

</details>


### [33] [AgentModernize: Preserving Business Logic in Legacy Modernization with Multi-Agent LLMs and Behavioral Specification Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17535)
*Sheikh Nazib Ahmed,Marnim Galib*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出AgentModernize多智能体框架，通过行为规范图(BSG)将遗留系统现代化转化为行为保持问题，在电信和银行场景中实现非零业务等价率(BER)，而传统方法(SP-LLM, CoT-LLM)在所有骨干模型下BER均为0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具和基于LLM的方法将遗留系统现代化视为语法翻译，忽略了隐式规则、边界情况处理和跨模块约束，导致业务逻辑丢失。

Method: 提出多智能体框架AgentModernize，包含四个专用智能体（提取、规范、代码生成、验证），核心中间产物是行为规范图(BSG)，强制业务逻辑在被提取后、代码生成前显式化和可检查。

Result: 在LegacyModernize-8基准上，完整AgentModernize在所有骨干模型下均取得非零平均BER（GPT-4o-mini 9.4%，GPT-4o 8.1%，GPT-5.3-codex 19.4%），而SP-LLM和CoT-LLM在所有场景下BER均为0%。BSG捕获了91.2%的金标准规则。

Conclusion: 遗留系统现代化的瓶颈是代码生成而非业务逻辑提取，BSG能有效捕获绝大多数业务规则。

Abstract: Legacy modernization breaks business logic. Most tools and LLM-based approaches treat modernization as syntax translation, losing implicit rules, edge-case handling, and cross-module constraints. We present AgentModernize, a multi-agent framework that treats modernization as a behavioral preservation problem. Four specialized agents handle extraction, specification, code generation, and validation. The key intermediate artifact -- a Behavioral Specification Graph (BSG) -- forces extracted business logic to be explicit and inspectable before any code is generated. We evaluated on LegacyModernize-8, eight scenarios spanning telecom and banking, using three models (GPT-4o-mini, GPT-4o, GPT-5.3-codex) under a fair protocol: same gold-standard tests, 3 trials, temperature 0.0. Full AgentModernize with feedback was the only configuration with non-zero mean BER under every backbone. SP-LLM and CoT-LLM scored 0.0% on every scenario, on every backbone. AgentModernize without feedback scored 0.0% mean BER with GPT-4o-mini and GPT-5.3-codex; under GPT-4o it achieved non-zero BER only on S1 (44.4%; 5.6% mean over scenarios). Mean BER for full AgentModernize was 9.4% (mini), 8.1% (GPT-4o), and 19.4% (codex). The BSG captures 91.2% of gold-standard rules, confirming that the bottleneck is code generation, not extraction.

</details>


### [34] [Rethinking Code Review in the Age of AI: A Vision for Agentic Code Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17548)
*Hüseyin Özgür Kamalı,Erdem Tuna,Vahid Haratian,Eray Tüzün*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文回顾了代码审查的历史演变，分析了LLM和AI智能体带来的变革，提出了一个包含五个阶段（PR创建、增强、审稿人选择、AI辅助审查、PR回顾）的AI驱动代码审查工作流框架，并讨论了可靠性、偏见、隐私等开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码助手提高了代码生产速度，但导致需要审查的代码量激增，代码审查成为瓶颈；现有的AI支持工具碎片化，缺乏端到端的PR审查工作流支持。

Method: 历史文献综述+愿景框架设计，提出结合专业AI智能体与人工控制质量门的五阶段工作流框架。

Result: 提出了一个AI驱动的端到端代码审查工作流框架，明确了五个阶段及人类在关键决策点的保留角色，并识别了负责任采用的主要开放挑战。

Conclusion: 需要更有效的人机协作来解决AI时代代码审查的瓶颈问题，并提出了未来研究议程。

Abstract: Code review has evolved for decades, from informal peer checking to today's pull request (PR) workflows, yet it remains a largely manual, uneven, and cognitively demanding process. The rise of Artificial Intelligence (AI) coding assistants has intensified this challenge: while these tools increase code production velocity, they also expand the volume of code requiring review, turning code review into a growing bottleneck. Current AI support remains fragmented, with tools focusing on isolated tasks such as reviewer recommendation, PR description generation, or comment suggestion rather than the end-to-end PR review workflow. In this paper, we review the historical evolution of code review practices and examine the shift driven by large language models (LLMs) and agentic AI systems. We then present a vision for an AI-powered code review workflow combining specialized agents with human-controlled quality gates. Our framework spans five stages: PR Creation, PR Augmentation, Reviewer Selection, AI-Assisted Code Review, and PR Retrospective, with humans retained at key decision points to preserve judgment, accountability, and team-level understanding. We identify major open challenges for responsible adoption, including reliability, bias, privacy, automation bias, transparency, and evaluation, and offer a research agenda for more effective human-AI collaboration in software engineering.

</details>


### [35] [Firefly: Illuminating Large-Scale Verified Tool-Call Data Generation from Real APIs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17558)
*Yuxuan Lu,Ziyi Wang,Yingzhou Lu,Yisi Sang,Jiri Gesi,Xianfeng Tang,Yimeng Zhang,Zhenwei Dai,Hui Liu,Hanqing Lu,Chen Luo,Qi He,Benoit Dumoulin,Jing Huang,Dakuo Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: FireFly是一个从真实MCP服务器生成经过验证的工具调用数据的流水线，通过反转标准合成流程（先探索API再逆向生成任务）确保标签正确性，并构建检索增强模拟器解决环境漂移问题。4B模型在FireFly上训练后，在多个工具调用基准上达到Claude Sonnet 4.6水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的工具调用智能体训练数据要么在合成环境中生成（与真实API行为脱节），要么生成的任务缺乏可验证的真实结果标签。需要一种能从真实API环境生成可验证数据的方法。

Method: 1) 反转标准合成流程：先让强LLM沿图引导的DAG结构探索真实API，然后从观察到的结果反向合成任务，保证标签正确性；2) 构建成对工具图并采样子DAG以处理约1000个工具的大规模真实工具空间；3) 构建检索增强模拟器缓存探索结果并在训练评估时重放，实现离线可复现的强化学习。

Result: 生成了5,144个经过验证的任务，覆盖240个服务器和993个工具。一个4B参数模型（使用GRPO在FireFly上训练）在保留测试集上匹配Claude Sonnet 4.6，并在Tau2-Bench、MCPMark、MCP-Atlas等多个工具调用基准上表现出改进。

Conclusion: FireFly通过从真实API逆向生成可验证数据的流水线，有效解决了工具调用智能体训练数据缺乏真实性和可验证性的问题，小模型也能达到大模型水平。

Abstract: Training tool-calling agents requires large-scale trajectory data with verifiable labels, yet existing approaches either synthesize environments that diverge from real API behavior or generate tasks without ground-truth outcomes for verification. We present FireFly, a pipeline for generating verified tool-call data from real-world MCP servers. Our key insight is to invert the standard synthesis pipeline: rather than generating tasks and hoping they are solvable, we first let a strong LLM explore real APIs along graph-guided DAG structures, then synthesize tasks backward from observed outcomes, guaranteeing label correctness by construction. To handle the scale of real-world tool spaces (${\sim}$1,000 tools), we build a pairwise tool graph and sample sub-DAGs to focus exploration on semantically coherent workflows. To address environment drift in live APIs, we construct a retrieval-augmented simulator that caches all exploration results and replays them during training and evaluation, enabling fully offline and reproducible RL. Applying this pipeline yields 5,144 verified tasks spanning 240 servers and 993 tools. A 4B-parameter model trained with GRPO on FireFly matches Claude Sonnet 4.6 on our held-out test set and shows improvements on multiple tool-calling benchmarks including Tau2-Bench, MCPMark, and MCP-Atlas.

</details>


### [36] [Contextualized Code Pretraining for Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17957)
*Chen Liu,Qingyuan Liang,Hanwen Zhang,Zeyu Sun,Yakun Zhang,Lu Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了一种上下文感知的代码预训练框架 CallerGen，通过静态分析提取调用者-被调用者关系，在训练和评估中引入调用上下文，使小模型在真实仓库场景的代码生成任务中超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码模型主要在"自然语言描述→代码"的数据上训练，但实际开发中开发者常基于项目上下文（调用上下文）来实现缺失函数，而现有模型未被优化以可靠利用这种上下文信息。

Method: 提出 invocation-aware 框架，通过静态分析从真实仓库自动提取大规模 caller-callee 对，构建预训练任务和评估基准，使代码生成条件化于调用上下文。训练了 CallerGen 系列模型（多规模），并构建 CallerEval 基准。

Result: CallerGen 的 220M 和 0.5B 模型在 CallerEval 上分别达到 16.58% 和 22.81% pass@1，超越同规模基线，且与更大模型竞争力相当。

Conclusion: 调用上下文在真实仓库场景的代码生成中至关重要，invocation-aware 预训练能显著提升模型在仓库级别代码生成任务中的表现。

Abstract: As code generation becomes increasingly central to improving software development efficiency, modern code models are largely trained and evaluated on code with natural-language descriptions. In real projects, developers often implement missing functions under limited project-specific artifacts, while the local call-site context is already available in the surrounding code. This usage context provides actionable cues about expected behavior, but existing models are not explicitly optimized to leverage it reliably, leading to implementations that may not integrate smoothly with surrounding usage in repository settings.
  In this work, we propose contextualized code pretraining, an invocation-aware framework that integrates calling context into both the training and evaluation of code models. Using static analysis, we automatically extract large-scale caller-callee pairs from real repositories to construct pretraining tasks and benchmarks that condition generation on the calling context. We train CallerGen, the first code models pretrained with invocation-aware objectives spanning multiple sizes, and evaluate them on CallerEval, a new benchmark featuring realistic scenarios. Experiments show that CallerGen outperforms comparable-scale models and remains competitive with larger ones across two benchmarks. Our 220M and 0.5B models achieve 16.58% and 22.81@% pass1, surpassing baselines on CallerEval. These results highlight the importance of calling context in realistic code generation.

</details>


### [37] [BLAgent: Agentic RAG for File-Level Bug Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17965)
*Md Afif Al Mamun,Gias Uddin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: BLAgent 是一个面向文件级缺陷定位的智能体 RAG 框架，通过代码结构感知的仓库编码、双视角查询变换和两阶段智能体重排序，在 SWE-bench Lite 上达到 78%+ Top-1 准确率，并将修复成功率提升 20% 以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文件级缺陷定位是下游软件维护任务的关键瓶颈，现有 RAG 管道依赖静态检索，缺乏识别错误代码所需的推理能力，且错误会传播到下游阶段。

Method: 提出 BLAgent 框架，包含三个核心设计：(i) 带有路径增强 AST 分块的代码结构感知仓库编码，(ii) 捕获结构信号和行为信号的双视角查询变换，(iii) 结合符号检查与证据推理的两阶段智能体重排序，在紧凑候选集上执行有界推理。

Result: 在 SWE-bench Lite 上，BLAgent 使用开源模型达到 78%+ Top-1 准确率，使用闭源模型达到 86%+，同时比最强基线便宜 18 倍以上；集成到 APR 框架后，端到端修复成功率提升 20%+。

Conclusion: BLAgent 通过智能体 RAG 方法有效解决了文件级缺陷定位问题，在准确率和成本之间取得了良好平衡，并能显著提升下游自动程序修复的效果。

Abstract: Bug localization remains a key bottleneck in downstream software maintenance tasks, including root cause analysis, triage, and automated program repair (APR), despite recent advances in large language model (LLM)-based repair systems. File-level bug localization is especially critical in hierarchical pipelines, where errors can propagate to downstream stages such as statement-level localization or patch generation. While Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) offers a promising direction for grounding LLMs in repository context, existing RAG pipelines rely on static retrieval and lack the reasoning needed to identify faulty code accurately. In this work, we present BLAgent, a novel agentic RAG framework for file-level bug localization that integrates three key ideas: (i) code structure-aware repository encoding with path-augmented AST-based chunking, (ii) dual-perspective query transformation capturing both structural and behavioral signals, and (iii) two-phase agentic reranking combining symbolic inspection with evidence-grounded reasoning. Unlike prior graph-based or multi-hop agentic approaches, BLAgent performs bounded reasoning over a compact candidate set, balancing accuracy and cost. On SWE-bench Lite, BLAgent attains over 78% Top-1 accuracy with open-source models and over 86% with a closed-source model, while being over 18x cheaper than the strongest baseline using the same model. When integrated into an APR framework, it improves end-to-end repair success by over 20%.

</details>


### [38] [CommitDistill: A Lightweight Knowledge-Centric Memory Layer for Software Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18284)
*Divya Chukkapalli,Thejesh Avula,Aditya Aggarwal,Harsimran Singh,Amith Tallanki*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CommitDistill是一个开源Python原型工具，通过确定性正则表达式将本地git历史挖掘为类型化知识单元（Facts, Skills, Patterns），使用TF-IDF检索器并带校准静默阈值（theta=2.5）。在五个公共仓库（25000 commits, 1167个单元）上评估，有用精度0.525，在256字符预算下命中率0.750，优于BM25的0.333和git log --grep的0.083，但在LLM评判的时间旅行bug修复任务中未发现统计显著的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件仓库中的提交信息、PR讨论和Issue线程含有大量非结构化知识，但开发者和AI编码助手很少有效重用这些历史信息。现有LLM智能体记忆架构（MemGPT、生成式智能体等）表明智能体记忆应该是蒸馏后的类型化知识，而非原始交互文本，作者将该思路适配到软件仓库的git历史中。

Method: 提出CommitDistill，一个确定性、无依赖、纯本地、无需嵌入的Python原型。使用确定性正则表达式从git历史中提取类型化知识单元（Facts, Skills, Patterns），通过TF-IDF检索器配合校准的静默阈值（theta=2.5）实现分布外查询的弃权机制，输出为可检查的纯JSON存储。

Result: 在五个公共仓库（25000 commits, 1167个单元）上，有用精度0.525，Cohen's kappa=0.633；256字符预算下命中率0.750，优于BM25的0.333和git log --grep的0.083；10000 commits提取耗时<4秒；但在200个时间旅行bug修复的LLM评判中，无条件产生统计显著提升。

Conclusion: CommitDistill在预算受限的检索场景下效果显著，但在端到端LLM辅助任务中未表现出统计显著的收益，CD-Hybrid与BM25也无显著差异。工具在效率上具有优势（4秒处理10000 commits），但实际效用有待进一步验证。

Abstract: Software repositories accumulate large amounts of unstructured knowledge in commit messages, pull-request discussions, and issue threads, but developers and AI coding assistants rarely reuse this history effectively. Recent work on typed-memory architectures for LLM agents (MemGPT, generative agents, and the PlugMem module of Yang et al.) argues that agent memory should be distilled, typed knowledge rather than raw interaction text. We adapt that stance to a software repository's own git history under a constrained regime: deterministic, dependency-free, local-only, no embeddings. We present CommitDistill, an open-source Python prototype that mines a local git history into typed knowledge units (Facts, Skills, Patterns) using deterministic regex and surfaces them through a TF-IDF retriever with a calibrated silence threshold (theta = 2.5) that abstains on out-of-distribution queries. The artefact is a trust-instrumented memory substrate: deterministic, no external service, inspectable plain-JSON store, tunable abstention. A case study on five public repositories spanning Python, JavaScript, C, and Java (25,000 commits, 1,167 extracted units) reports useful-precision 0.525 at Cohen's kappa = 0.633 on 40 dual-annotated Python units. The decisive finding is budget-constrained retrieval: at a 256-character per-query budget, CommitDistill reaches 0.750 hit-rate on a 12-query benchmark against BM25's 0.333 and git log --grep's 0.083. On a four-arm paired LLM-as-judge evaluation (n=200 time-travel bug-fixes, two judges) covering control, CommitDistill, a body-budget-matched CD-Hybrid, and BM25, no condition produces a statistically detectable lift over control on the headline mean and CD-Hybrid is indistinguishable from BM25 head-to-head. Extraction over 10,000 commits completes in under 4 seconds on a laptop. Source, annotations, baselines, and a reproducibility script accompany this paper.

</details>


### [39] [One Developer Is All You Need: A Case Study of an AI-Augmented One-Person Squad in a Brownfield Enterprise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18461)
*Marcelo Vilas Boas,Gustavo Pinto,Edward Roberto Monteiro,Vinicius Fernandes Carida,Danilo Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过案例研究展示了一名资深工程师在四位AI智能体支持下，采用规范驱动开发工作流，在受监管企业环境中单人完成了原本需要四人团队的产品开发，时间减半、代码首次审查通过率90%、集成测试全部通过、人员成本降低85%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI工具使工程师能够承担原本跨职能团队的角色，但缺乏关于如何在受监管企业环境中设计或评估这种"一人团队"模式的系统性证据，组织在采用该模式时缺乏指导。

Method: 案例研究：一名高级工程师在Spec-Driven Development工作流下，由四个AI Agent辅助，交付原本为四人团队设计的棕地产品项目。

Result: 开发时间减半，AI生成代码首次审查通过率90%，集成测试全部通过，直接人员成本降低85%以上。

Conclusion: AI并非替代团队成员，而是倍增了留下的经验丰富工程师的产出；规范质量和机构知识（而非模型能力）是"一人团队"成功的关键约束。

Abstract: AI tools are enabling engineers to absorb roles previously distributed across cross-functional squads, yet there is little structured evidence on how to design or evaluate such a one-person squad in a regulated enterprise setting. Without that evidence, organizations adopting this model lack guidance on which design decisions make it viable and which conditions cause it to break down. We report a case study in which a single staff engineer, supported by four AI agents under a Spec-Driven Development workflow, delivered a brownfield product initiative scoped for a four-person squad in half the planned time, with 90\% acceptance of AI-generated code on first review, full integration test pass rates, and an above-85\% reduction in direct staffing cost. The results indicate that AI does not replace team members it multiplies the throughput of the experienced engineer who remains, making specification quality and institutional knowledge, not model capability, the binding constraints on one-person squad success.

</details>


### [40] [Overeager Coding Agents: Measuring Out-of-Scope Actions on Benign Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18583)
*Yubin Qu,Ying Zhang,Yanjun Zhang,Gelei Deng,Yuekang Li,Leo Yu Zhang,Yi Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文指出当前编码智能体在自主运行时存在"过度积极操作"问题——即在执行用户良性请求时超出授权范围执行额外操作（如删除无关文件、修改配置等）。为此构建了OverEager-Bench基准测试集，包含500个验证场景和约7500次运行测试，覆盖4种智能体产品和6个基础模型。研究发现，去除许可声明文本会使过度积极率从0.0%飙升到17.1%；框架层面的权限门控机制对行为影响最大，其中"请求续行"框架（OpenHands）表现最好（0.2-4.5%），而许可框架下基础模型差异可达15.9个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体在自主执行时存在超出用户授权范围的过度行为，这是一个不同于传统能力故障或提示注入的新型授权问题，但目前缺乏系统性的基准测试和研究工具。

Method: 构建OverEager-Bench基准测试集，采用行为梯度验证器确保场景判别力，通过双通道栈（PATH注入垫片+代理事件流）审计内部工具调用，同时提供consent_kept和consent_stripped两个版本以区分授权声明对行为的影响。

Result: 在Claude Code上，去除许可声明使过度积极率从0.0%升至17.1%。500个场景和约7500次运行显示，每个共享基础模型上去除许可声明都使过度积极率增加11.9-17.2个百分点。框架轴主导效应：许可型框架（5.4-27.7%）vs 请求续行框架（0.2-4.5%）。同一框架内基础模型差异达15.9个百分点。

Conclusion: 编码智能体的过度积极行为是一个严重且被低估的授权问题，需要更好的权限门控设计和安全对齐机制。框架层面的权限设计比模型层面的对齐更关键地影响智能体的行为边界。

Abstract: Coding agents now run autonomously with shell, file, and network privileges. When a user issues a benign request, the agent sometimes does more than asked: it deletes unrelated files, wipes a stale credentials backup, or rewrites configuration the user never mentioned. We call these scope expansions overeager actions, an authorization problem distinct from capability failures, prompt injection, or sandbox escapes.
  We present OverEager-Gen, a benchmark dedicated to overeager behavior on benign tasks. Building it surfaces a measurement-validity issue: if a benchmark spells out the authorized scope inside the prompt, the agent stops inferring boundaries and starts pattern-matching declaration text. On Claude Code, stripping the consent declaration alone raises the overeager rate from 0.0% to 17.1% on paired scenarios (McNemar exact p = 2.4 x 10^-4). OverEager-Gen therefore certifies each scenario's discriminative power before admission via a behavioral-gradient validator, audits internal tool calls through a dual-channel stack (PATH-injected shim plus per-agent event streams), and ships byte-identical consent_kept and consent_stripped variants.
  OverEager-Bench contains 500 validated scenarios and ~7,500 runs across four agent products (Claude Code, OpenHands, Codex CLI, Gemini CLI) and six base models; a 50-sample re-annotation gives Cohen's kappa = 0.73 and rule-judge recall = 1.00. Stripping consent multiplies the overeager rate on every shared base model (Delta in [11.9, 17.2] pp). The framework axis dominates effect size: a permissive cluster (Claude Code, Codex CLI, Gemini CLI) runs at 5.4-27.7% while the ask-to-continue framework (OpenHands) sits at 0.2-4.5% (Fisher p <= 10^-5). Within-framework base-model variance reaches 15.9 pp, indicating that model-layer alignment does not fully propagate through permissive permission gating.

</details>

### [41] [Exceeding the Numerical and Performance Characteristics of IEEE-754 SGEMM with BFloat16 Tensor Cores on GPUs for Scientific Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16617)
*Harun Bayraktar,Cole Brower,John Gunnels,Greg Henry,Cherin Joseph,Jack Kosaian,Dmitry Lyakh,Lukas Mosimann,Victor Podlozhnyuk,Addison Richards,Paul Springer,Haicheng Wu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 研究利用GPU上的BF16 Tensor Core模拟FP32矩阵乘法，在Blackwell架构上利用BF16与FP32相同的动态范围、全速FP32累加器以及集成缩放硬件等特性，实现了超越原生FP32的数值和性能表现，并提供了完整的库级实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低精度浮点计算资源（主要用于AI）因其数值强度和能效优势而快速增长，远超高精度计算资源。因此需要利用充足的低精度硬件来模拟更高精度的数学计算，以在科学计算等场景中充分利用硬件资源。

Method: 利用bfloat16 (BF16) Tensor Core在Blackwell GPU架构上的特性（与FP32相同的动态范围、全速FP32累加器、集成缩放硬件），通过BF16模拟实现FP32矩阵乘法，并针对非规格化数进行处理。

Result: BF16模拟的FP32矩阵乘法在数值特性和性能特性上均超越了原生FP32实现，适用于科学计算应用。

Conclusion: 利用低精度BF16硬件模拟高精度FP32计算是可行且高效的方案，提供了完整的库级实现，对科学计算领域具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Largely due to their increased native capacity for numerical intensity and power efficiency, reduced-precision floating-point computing resources, primarily used in artificial intelligence (AI) applications, have expanded at a greater rate than their higher-precision relatives. This has led to various efforts focused upon leveraging plentiful reduced-precision hardware to mimic higher-precision mathematical calculations. This paper studies a specific use case, namely the use of bfloat16 (BF16) Tensor Cores found on modern GPUs in service of single precision (FP32) matrix multiply operations. Given that BF16 and FP32 share the same dynamic range, the option to accumulate BF16 operations into FP32 accumulators (at full-speed), and additional BF16 arithmetic characteristics specific to the Blackwell GPU architecture, such as integrated scaling hardware, such emulation is highly motivated. This paper examines the performance, efficiency, power, and numerical characteristics of FP32 matrix multiplication via BF16-based emulation and demonstrates how it exceeds numerical and performance characteristics of native FP32 for scientific applications. We also discuss a full library-ready implementation that correctly deals with denormals.

</details>


### [42] [HexAGenT: Efficient Agentic LLM Serving via Workflow- and Heterogeneity-Aware Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16637)
*You Peng,Youhe Jiang,Wenshuang Li,Xu Xu,Ke Zhou,Jiawei Jiang,Chen Wang,Binhang Yuan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出HexAGenT，一种针对异构预填充-解码分离LLM服务集群的工作流感知调度器，用于优化在线智能体工作流的端到端延迟，在A100/H100/H200集群上相比基线实现20-33%的SLO缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体LLM应用越来越多地以多步工作流形式执行用户请求，涉及规划、工具调用、分支、优化和合成等步骤，用户感知的是整个工作流的端到端延迟而非单次LLM调用的延迟。现有调度方法缺乏对工作流级延迟目标的考虑，且未针对异构预填充-解码分离集群进行优化。

Method: HexAGenT将每个请求建模为在线揭示的DAG，维护工作流独立完成时间线的运行估计，根据错过该时间线的预测风险对就绪调用进行优先级排序，并联合选择预填充放置、解码放置和本地队列优先级，同时考虑KV-cache容量和跨阶段传输延迟。

Result: 在代表性智能体工作负载和异构A100/H100/H200集群上，HexAGenT在95%达成率下平均减少20.1%的SLO规模，在99%达成率下平均减少33.0%，最大减少分别达到45.0%和80.5%。

Conclusion: HexAGenT通过工作流感知调度和异构资源协同优化，显著提升了智能体工作流的端到端延迟性能，证明了在异构预填充-解码分离集群上进行工作流级调度的有效性。

Abstract: Agentic LLM applications increasingly execute user requests as multi-step workflows involving planning, tool use, branching, refinement, and synthesis. In such settings, users experience the end-to-end latency of an entire workflow, not the latency of any single LLM call. In this paper, we study how to schedule online agentic workflows across heterogeneous prefill-decode disaggregated LLM serving clusters to efficiently meet workflow-level latency objectives. The problem is challenging because workflow dependencies are revealed incrementally at runtime, calls have heterogeneous prompts, outputs, and KV-cache requirements, and the prefill and decode stages impose different compute, memory, and transfer constraints across heterogeneous GPUs. To solve this problem, we present HexAGenT, a workflow-aware scheduler for a heterogeneous prefill-decode inference service. HexAGenT models each request as an online-revealed DAG, maintains a running estimate of the workflow's standalone completion horizon, prioritizes ready calls by projected risk of missing that horizon, and jointly selects prefill placement, decode placement, and local queue priority while accounting for KV-cache capacity and cross-stage transfer latency. Across representative agentic workloads and heterogeneous A100/H100/H200 clusters, HexAGenT reduces the SLO scale required for timely workflow completion by an average of 20.1% at 95% attainment and 33.0% at 99% attainment, with maximum reductions of 45.0% and 80.5%, respectively.

</details>


### [43] [Approximate Distributed Coded Computing: Polynomial Codes and Randomized Sketching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16744)
*Neophytos Charalambides,Arya Mazumdar*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文综述了编码计算（通过冗余克服分布式系统瓶颈）与随机数值线性代数（通过概率方法压缩加速线性代数运算）两大领域的基础，并展示了结合两者技术的分布式方案，用于在存在慢速或无响应服务器的情况下加速优化和机器学习算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模分布式系统中存在慢速或无响应服务器的瓶颈问题，需要同时利用编码计算的冗余容错能力和随机数值线性代数的压缩加速能力，以更高效地执行优化和机器学习任务。

Method: 综述并整合编码计算（Coded Computing）与随机数值线性代数（Randomized Numerical Linear Algebra）两领域的理论基础，提出结合冗余编码与随机压缩的分布式计算方案。

Result: 展示了结合编码计算与随机数值线性代数的分布式方案能够有效应对慢速或无响应服务器问题，加速优化和机器学习算法。

Conclusion: 编码计算和随机数值线性代数的结合为分布式系统中的性能优化提供了有效途径，对大规模机器学习和优化任务具有重要价值。

Abstract: Coded computing is a distributed paradigm that uses coding theory to introduce \textit{redundancy} and overcome bottlenecks in large-scale systems. In the same vein, randomized numerical linear algebra employs probabilistic methods to \textit{compress} and accelerate linear algebraic operations, addressing challenges in high-dimensional data analysis. This article reviews the foundations of both fields and presents distributed schemes that combine techniques from both to speed up optimization and machine learning algorithms, in the presence of slow or non-responsive servers. Along the way, we touch on various related topics and mathematical concepts.

</details>


### [44] [GoodServe: Towards High-Goodput Serving of Agentic LLM Inferences over Heterogeneous Resources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16867)
*Boxiao Du,Boning Huangfu,Yizhou Luo,Chen Chen,Zijun Li,Minchen Yu,Xiaoyi Fan,Minyi Guo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: GoodServe是一个面向异构GPU集群的agentic推理服务系统，通过预测输出长度和GPU状态，采用"just-enough"实例选择启发式算法进行推理路由，并辅以运行时请求迁移，以最大化满足端到端延迟SLO的goodput。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在agentic应用中，LLM推理的及时完成至关重要，而推理请求需要在异构GPU资源池中路由到合适的GPU以满足延迟要求，现有方法未能充分优化goodput。

Method: 采用"预测-纠正"两阶段方法：1）准确且实用地估计请求输出长度和GPU服务状态；2）基于供需信息，使用"just-enough"实例选择启发式算法进行路由决策；3）定期监控活跃请求的SLO违规风险，触发运行时请求迁移以应对动态变化。

Result: 相比现有路由方法，GoodServe的goodput提升最高达27.4%。

Conclusion: GoodServe通过感知异构资源的智能路由和运行时迁移，显著优化了agentic推理服务的goodput。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) play a critical role in emerging agentic applications, where the timely completion of each entire inference is critical. Meanwhile, agentic LLM inferences are increasingly served on heterogeneous GPUs in operator's resource pools. Therefore, it is crucial to route incoming inference requests to appropriate GPUs so that their end-to-end latency requirements are satisfied whenever possible, thereby achieving high goodput. In this paper, we propose GoodServe, a goodput-optimized serving system for agentic inferences over heterogeneous resources. GoodServe performs inference routing in a predict-and-rectify manner. It estimates the request output lengths as well as the GPU serving status in an accurate and also practical manner. Based on information from both the demand and resource sides, it then makes high-quality routing decisions using a just-enough instance selection heuristic. It also periodically monitors SLO-violation risks of active requests and triggers runtime request migrations to address unexpected dynamics. Our evaluations show that GoodServe improves goodput by up to 27.4% over existing routing methods.

</details>


### [45] [Charon: A Unified and Fine-Grained Simulator for Large-Scale LLM Training and Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17164)
*Mengtian Yang,Zhekun Zhang,Mingheng Wu,Jianwen Yan,Hanshi Sun,Li-wen Chang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Charon是一个统一、模块化、细粒度的LLM性能模拟器，能够在训练和推理场景下高精度预测性能（误差低于5.35%），并帮助发现更优配置以提升实际系统吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模LLM训练和推理的部署面临并行策略、系统优化和硬件配置的复杂设计空间，准确且快速的性能模拟对于指导优化和系统研究至关重要。

Method: 提出Charon模拟器，采用统一、模块化和细粒度的架构，对LLM在各种配置下的性能进行精确预测。

Result: Charon在多种模型和配置下预测误差始终低于5.35%，在大规模GPU集群训练场景下误差低于3.74%；在一个实际推理部署案例中，发现了优于工程调优基线的配置方案。 

Conclusion: Charon作为高精度LLM性能模拟器，能有效指导优化和系统研究，具有显著的实用价值。

Abstract: Deploying large-scale LLM training and inference with optimal performance is exceptionally challenging due to a complex design space of parallelism strategies, system optimizations, and hardware configurations. Accurate and rapid performance simulation is critical for guiding optimization efforts and system studies by validating "what-if" Hooker Figure hypotheses. To address this, we introduce Charon, a unified, modular, and fine-grained simulator for accurately predicting LLM performance. Experiments show Charon achieves high accuracy across different models and configurations, with an overall prediction error consistently under 5.35%, and even under 3.74% for training with a large-scale GPU cluster. In a practical inference deployment case, Charon discovered a configuration that improved system throughput over an engineering-tuned baseline, demonstrating its significant real-world value.

</details>


### [46] [Send: Objects, History, and Transactions in a Single-Verb Kernel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17401)
*Christopher Goes*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出一个最小内核，通过统一的消息发送接口、事务和ephemeral/persistent对象区分，在系统层面同时保证六个结构属性（认证来源、不透明封装、原子多对象提交、确定性重放、不可变历史、历史衍生状态），而非通过分层组合现有机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统通过分层组合（RPC、能力ACL、事务协调器、事件日志等）实现多对象协调的六个结构属性，但这种组合方式脆弱且复杂，需要更底层的统一内核设计。

Method: 基于s-expression构建最小内核，提供统一的'send'接口、事务支持，以及ephemeral（继承调用者上下文）与persistent（切换到内核分配的身份和追加日志）的对象区分。内核通过类型化分发自动将每个send目标归入六种情况之一。

Result: 在内核忠实信任下，六个属性均在内核层面得到保证；在不透明的操作者场景下，五个属性仍能存活。追加日志支撑不可变性和重放，持久分发提供认证来源和不透明性，事务提供原子协调。

Conclusion: 该内核设计使得多对象协调的六个属性从分层组合的脆弱机制变为内核层面的结构保证，且可通过加密编译器实现对抗操作者的部署。

Abstract: Multi-party object coordination - across object-capability systems, smart-contract platforms, distributed actors, and event-sourced architectures - is shaped by six structural properties: authenticated provenance, opaque encapsulation, atomic multi-object commit, deterministic replay, immutable history, and history-derived state. Existing systems compose subsets via separate layered mechanisms (RPC, capability ACLs, transaction coordinators, event journals, vat boundaries); each layer is well-studied but the combination is fragile. We present a minimal kernel which makes them jointly compatible.
  Our kernel is built from s-expressions, a uniform 'send' interface, transactions, and one primitive object distinction: *ephemeral* (caller's context inherited) vs. *persistent* (context switches to the target's kernel-assigned identity and append-only log). The kernel structurally classifies every send target into one of six cases without input from the caller - uniform caller interface, intensional kernel dispatch.
  Under kernel-faithful trust (the kernel runs its semantics as specified), this design holds all six properties as *kernel-level* against arbitrary programs - the kernel's transition function refuses states violating them. Opacity *against the operator* additionally requires operator-faithful trust (the operator accesses logs only via 'recall' and does not censor or reorder transactions); under kernel-faithful alone, five of six guarantees survive an unconstrained operator. Append-only logs underpin immutability, replay, and history-derived state; kernel-controlled persistent dispatch yields authenticated provenance and opacity; transactions deliver atomic coordination. Operator-adversarial deployments can be realized with a cryptographic compiler.

</details>


### [47] [TierCheck: Tiered Checkpointing for Fault Tolerance in Large Language Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17821)
*Shujie Han,Feng Jiang,Patrick P. C. Lee,Xiao Zhang,Zhijie Huang,Nannan Zhao,Xiaonan Zhao,Lichen Pan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: TierCheck 是一种集群感知的分层检查点系统，通过三层级存储（本地内存、对等内存、远程持久存储）结合故障异质性，实现低开销、高频率的 LLM 训练检查点，支持快速局部恢复，端到端检查点时间低于 10 秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 训练频繁遭遇异构故障（从 GPU 崩溃到集群级中断），现有单层存储的检查点系统无法兼顾低开销保存和快速恢复，需要一种能对齐存储层级与故障类型的解决方案。

Method: 三层级设计：轻量差分检查点存放在本地和对等内存中用于快速局部恢复，重量级基检查点异步迁移到远程持久存储；严格全局一致性保证且不阻塞训练；集群感知的快速检查点恢复机制。

Result: 在 400 亿参数模型上验证，训练开销低，端到端检查点时间低于 10 秒，支持高频检查点，实现了低开销持久化与快速恢复之间的最优平衡。

Conclusion: TierCheck 通过分层存储策略有效解决了 LLM 训练中的检查点效率与恢复速度的矛盾，适用于大规模分布式训练场景。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) training is frequently interrupted by a heterogeneous spectrum of failures, from common GPU crashes to catastrophic cluster-wide outages. Existing checkpointing systems rely on monolithic, single-tier storage backend, forcing a trade-off between state-saving overhead and recovery speed. We propose TierCheck, a cluster-aware tiered checkpointing system that aligns storage placement with failure heterogeneity. TierCheck adopts a three-tier design that maintains lightweight differential checkpoints in local and peer memory for fast localized recovery, while asynchronously migrating heavyweight base checkpoints to remote persistent storage. It also ensures strict global consistency across tiers without stalling training, and achieves fast cluster-aware checkpoint restoration during recovery. Evaluations on models up to 40 billion parameters show that TierCheck achieves low training overhead, reduces end-to-end checkpointing time to under 10s, and supports high-frequency checkpointing, ultimately striking an optimal balance between low-overhead persistence and fast recovery.

</details>


### [48] [Guard: Scalable Straggler Detection and Node Health Management for Large-Scale Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17879)
*Guanliang Liu,Abhinandan Patni,Congzhu Lin,Zoe Zeng,Jack Wittmayer,Josh Wu,Ashvin Nihalani,Binxuan Huang,Yinghong Liu,Rory Na,Anthony Ko,Alexander Zhipa,Cong Cheng,Mi Sun,Vijay Rajakumar,Rejith George Joseph,Parthasarathy Govindarajen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Guard是一个可扩展的检测系统，通过轻量级在线性能监控和离线节点扫描机制，在大规模训练集群中识别掉队节点并确保节点健康，显著提升FLOPs利用率并降低训练步方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有健康检查机制（如NCCL测试、GPU烧机测试）主要关注功能正确性，无法检测"慢故障"（fail-slow）行为，导致训练性能悄然下降。训练数千GPU、持续数月的前沿基础模型时，微小性能退化会累积为巨大效率损失。

Method: 结合两类机制：1) 训练期间的轻量级在线性能监控，实时检测性能异常；2) 离线节点扫描机制，在节点投入生产工作负载前系统性地评估和认证节点健康状况。

Result: 部署在大规模基础模型预训练任务上，FLOPs利用率提升最高达1.7倍，训练步方差从20%降至1%，平均故障间隔时间（MTTF）增加，运营和调试开销显著降低。

Conclusion: 主动检测掉队节点和系统性节点认证对于维持大规模训练集群的稳定性和效率至关重要。

Abstract: Training frontier-scale foundation models involves coordinating tens of thousands of GPUs over multi-month runs, where even minor performance degradations can accumulate into substantial efficiency losses. Existing health-check mechanisms, such as NCCL tests or GPU burn-in, primarily focus on functional correctness and often fail to detect fail-slow behaviors that silently degrade system performance. In this paper, we present Guard, a scalable system for detecting stragglers and ensuring node health in large-scale training clusters. Guard combines lightweight online performance monitoring during training with an offline node-sweep mechanism that systematically evaluates and qualifies nodes before they participate in production workloads. This design enables Guard to detect both acute failures and long-running fail-slow behaviors that traditional diagnostics cannot capture. Deployed on large-scale foundation model pretraining workloads, Guard improves mean FLOPs utilization by up to 1.7x, reduces run-to-run training step variance from 20% to 1%, increases mean time to failure (MTTF), and significantly reduces operational and debugging overhead. These results demonstrate that proactive straggler detection and systematic node qualification are critical for maintaining stable and efficient large-scale training.

</details>


### [49] [AdaptiveLoad: Towards Efficient Video Diffusion Transformer Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17923)
*Yucheng Guo,Yongjian Guo,Zhong Guan,Haoran Sun,Wen Huang,Wanting Xu,Jing Long,Shuai Di,Junwu Xiong*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaptiveLoad框架，通过双约束自适应负载均衡系统和融合LayerNorm-Modulate CUDA核，解决视频扩散Transformer训练中因序列长度极端差异导致的GPU负载不均衡和利用率低的问题，在Wan 2.1世界模型上实现27.2%的训练吞吐提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于桶的数据加载策略采用"等token长度"约束，未考虑自注意力的二次复杂度，导致混合模态数据集中序列长度差异极大，造成严重的负载不均衡和GPU资源利用率低下。

Method: 提出AdaptiveLoad框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 双约束自适应负载均衡系统，通过同时限制内存消耗和计算负载(B×S^p ≤ M_comp)消除长序列瓶颈；(2) 融合LayerNorm-Modulate CUDA核，采用D-tile合并归约策略提升吞吐并缓解内存压力。

Result: 在Wan 2.1世界模型上，计算不均衡率从39%降至18.9%，峰值VRAM利用效率提升22.7%，整体训练吞吐提升27.2%。

Conclusion: AdaptiveLoad有效解决了视频扩散Transformer训练中的负载不均衡问题，显著提升了GPU资源利用率和训练效率。

Abstract: In video generation models, particularly world models, training large-scale video diffusion Transformers (such as DiT and MMDiT) poses significant computational challenges due to the extreme variance in sequence lengths within mixed-mode datasets. Existing bucket-based data loading strategies typically rely on "equal token length" constraints. This approach fails to account for the quadratic complexity of self-attention mechanisms, leading to severe load imbalance and underutilization of GPU resources.
  This paper proposes \textit{AdaptiveLoad}, an integrated optimization framework consisting of two core components: (1) A dual-constraint adaptive load balancing system, which eliminates long-sequence bottlenecks by simultaneously limiting memory consumption and computational load ($B \times S^p \le M_{\text{comp}}$); (2) A fused LayerNorm-Modulate CUDA kernel, which utilizes a D-tile coalesced reduction strategy to increase throughput and alleviate memory pressure. Experimental results on the Wan 2.1 world model demonstrate that our method reduces the computational imbalance rate from 39\% to 18.9\%, improves peak VRAM utilization efficiency by 22.7\%, and achieves an overall training throughput increase of 27.2\%.

</details>


### [50] [Duet instrumentation: An Agentic Approach to Improving Sensitivity in Cloud Service Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18397)
*Sebastian Koch,Nils Japke,David Bermbach*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为"duet instrumentation"的新型基准测试范式，利用大语言模型分析代码变更，在两个应用版本同步基准测试中直接测量性能相关变更处的性能差异，从而更灵敏地检测性能回归。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的应用基准测试将系统视为黑盒，难以检测性能回归；微基准测试虽更灵敏但实现成本高且无法捕捉集成系统整体性能。需要一种兼具两者优势的方法。

Method: 利用大语言模型分析两个连续应用版本间的代码变更，自动识别与性能相关的变更点，并在两个版本的同步基准测试中直接在这些变更处测量性能差异。

Result: 原型系统在生成检测与理想检测的对比中达到58%精确率、93%召回率和71%特异性（线距离阈值5）。在下游基准测试中，相比传统双应用基准测试，能以低至5倍的注入严重度检测到性能回归，同时保持相似的A/A延迟分布。

Conclusion: LLM启用的duet instrumentation范式能有效弥补应用基准测试的不足，实现更灵敏的性能回归检测，为云服务性能基准测试提供了新方向。

Abstract: Continuous cloud service performance benchmarking is essential for detecting performance bugs early before deploying them to production. However, detecting performance regressions using application benchmarks, which usually treat the system under test as a black box, is challenging due to variable I/O calls or changing performance characteristics of the underlying cloud infrastructure. Microbenchmarks are often more sensitive and accurate, but also more time-consuming to implement and run. Further, they do not capture the performance of the integrated system as a whole. A comprehensive performance assessment therefore typically requires a combination of both approaches. To address the shortcomings of application benchmarks, we propose duet instrumentation, a novel benchmarking paradigm enabled by recent advancements in large language model (LLM) code understanding. The idea is to analyze code changes between two consecutive application versions and measure performance differences directly at performance-relevant changes during a synchronized benchmark of both application versions, uncovering performance changes with higher sensitivity. We design a system that reliably automates the assessment and instrumentation of performance-relevant code changes between the two application versions. In experiments with a realistic testbed application offering configurable performance regressions, we find that our prototype achieves 58% precision, 93% recall, and 71% specificity (averaged across tasks) when comparing the generated instrumentation against the ideal instrumentation with a line-distance threshold of five. In the downstream application benchmark, we find that our prototype can detect performance regressions at up to 5x lower injected severity compared to a traditional duet application benchmark while preserving similar A/A latency distributions.

</details>


### [51] [JanusPipe: Efficient Pipeline Parallel Training for Machine Learning Interatomic Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18404)
*Hongyu Wang,Weijian Liu,Hongtao Xu,Yan Wang,Mingzhen Li,Weile Jia,Guangming Tan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: JanusPipe是一个针对保守性机器学习原子间势能模型（MLIPs）的高效3D并行训练系统，通过SymFold实现内存高效的流水线并行，通过WaveK减少流水线气泡，在32块GPU上平均吞吐量比1F1B和Hanayo分别提升1.51倍和1.45倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 保守性MLIPs的双反向传播执行模式与现有分布式训练系统（尤其是流水线并行）不匹配，缺乏可扩展且高效的分布式训练系统，限制了其规模化。

Method: 提出JanusPipe，一个3D并行（PP/DP/GP）训练系统。集成SymFold实现内存高效的流水线并行，设计WaveK通过平衡四阶段计算时间来减少流水线气泡。

Result: 在32块GPU上，JanusPipe的吞吐量平均比1F1B提升1.51倍，比Hanayo提升1.45倍。

Conclusion: JanusPipe有效解决了保守性MLIPs分布式训练的效率问题，为大规模分子动力学模拟提供了可行的训练基础设施。

Abstract: Discovering atom-level phenomena requires molecular dynamics (MD) simulations with ab initio accuracy. Machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) enable stable, high-accuracy MD simulations, and their models exhibit scaling-law trends similar to large language models. However, the lack of scalable and efficient distributed training systems for conservative MLIPs makes them difficult to scale. This is because conservative MLIPs inherently follow a double-backward execution pattern, which involves computing gradients during the forward pass. This pattern creates a mismatch with existing distributed training systems, especially for pipeline parallelism. Therefore, we present JanusPipe, an efficient 3D-parallel (PP/DP/GP) training system tailored for conservative MLIPs. It integrates SymFold to enable memory-efficient pipeline parallelism for conservative MLIPs, and WaveK to reduce pipeline bubbles by balancing the four-phase compute time. Experimental results on 32 GPUs show that JanusPipe improves throughput by $1.51\times$ and $1.45\times$ on average over 1F1B and Hanayo, respectively.

</details>


### [52] [CB-SpMV:A Data Aggregating and Balance Algorithm for Cache-Friendly Block-Based SpMV on GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18515)
*Xing Cong,Fukai Sun,Yifan Chen,Chenhao Xie*,Yi Liu,Depei Qian*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出CB-SpMV，一种面向GPU的缓存友好的稀疏矩阵向量乘优化算法，通过新颖的数据收敛自适应2D分块结构、块感知列聚合策略、子块格式选择和块间负载均衡算法，在2843个矩阵上对比cuSPARSE-BSR等方法实现了最高3.95倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SpMV在科学计算和机器学习中至关重要，但现有GPU优化方法在数据局部性、硬件利用率和负载均衡方面仍然存在不足，需要进一步优化。

Method: 提出CB-SpMV算法，核心包括：(1) 数据收敛自适应2D分块结构，将矩阵划分为独立子块，通过虚拟指针聚合块内数据以改善缓存局部性；(2) 块感知列聚合策略和子块格式选择以加速计算并适应不同稀疏矩阵；(3) 块间负载均衡算法以优化线程块间的任务分配。

Result: 在SuiteSparse Collection的2843个矩阵上评估，CB-SpMV显著提升了缓存命中率，在NVIDIA A100和RTX 4090 GPU上相对于cuSPARSE-BSR、TileSpMV和DASP等最先进方法实现了最高3.95倍的平均加速。

Conclusion: CB-SpMV通过创新的缓存友好设计有效提升了SpMV在GPU上的性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Sparse matrix-vector multiplication (SpMV) is crucial in computational science, engineering, and machine learning. Despite substantial efforts to improve SpMV performance on GPUs through various techniques, issues related to data locality, hardware utilization, and load balancing persist, leaving room for further optimization. This paper presents CB-SpMV, a cache-friendly SpMV optimization algorithm, using a novel data convergent and adaptable 2D blocking structure. The matrix in CB-SpMV is divided into independent sub-blocks, with virtual pointers aggregating different types of intra-block data for better cache-level data locality. To enhance hardware utilization, a block-aware column aggregation strategy and the selection of sub-block formats are proposed to accelerate computation and adapt to varying sparse matrices. Finally, an inter-block load-balancing algorithm is designed to ensure efficient workload distribution across thread blocks. Experimental evaluations on 2,843 matrices from the SuiteSparse Collection show that CB-SpMV significantly improves cache hit rates and achieves average speedups of up to 3.95x over state-of-the-art methods like cuSPARSE-BSR, TileSpMV, and DASP on NVIDIA A100 and RTX 4090 GPUs. The implementation is available at: \url{https://github.com/xing-cong/CB-Sparse}.

</details>


### [53] [EPIC: Abstraction and Polymorphism of In-Network Collectives on Ethernet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18683)
*Yitao Yuan,Jianglong Nie,Tianyu Bai,Ruizhe Zhou,Siyuan Cao,Xujie Fan,Yuchen Xu,Junkai Chen,Chenqi Zhao,Nengyuan Zhang,Shaoke Fang,Jiangyuan Chen,Yuanfeng Chen,Jiaqi Sun,Zhan Wang,Xiaohua Xu,Yuchao Zhang,Yang Liu,Xiangrui Yang,Jing Lin,Xiaohe Hu,Yang Li,Chao Jiang,Limin Xiao,Weifeng Zhang,Junjie Wang,Wei Cheng,Yazhu Lan,Jianbo Dong,Binzhang Fu,Wenfei Wu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: EPIC提出了一种基于"统一抽象、多态实现"原则的以太网内集合通信（INC）协议规范与参考系统，通过模块化设计、形式化验证和统一资源管理，解决跨层优化在开放以太网生态中的部署难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: INC加速对优化AI训练和推理有巨大潜力，但其跨层特性阻碍了在开放以太网生态中的投资和采用，亟需一种兼容标准以太网的方案来弥合这一鸿沟。

Method: 采用模块化设计支持从简单到复杂的渐进式硬件迭代；运用形式化验证方法证明多态模式的正确性；开发适用于多种INC场景的统一资源管理模型。

Result: 通过模型检查、包/流模拟、VM仿真、Tofino测试平台和FPGA/RTL验证等多种手段，证实了EPIC的正确性、性能优势和可行性。

Conclusion: EPIC为开放以太网生态提供了一种可行的INC方案，有望推动AI训练和推理的跨层优化落地。

Abstract: In-Network Collective (INC) acceleration holds immense potential for optimizing AI training and inference; however, its cross-layer nature has historically hindered investment and adoption within the open Ethernet ecosystem. To bridge this gap, we propose EPIC (Ethernet Polymorphic In-network Collective), an INC protocol specification and reference system built on the principle of "Unified Abstraction, Polymorphic Realization." EPIC introduces an abstraction compatible with standard Ethernet that aligns functional boundaries with participant roles, while offering polymorphic realizations tailored to varying hardware capabilities.
  We address three fundamental challenges: first, we employ a modular design that enables an evolutionary path from simple to complex implementations, allowing vendors to iterate their hardware incrementally; second, we apply formal verification methodologies to prove the correctness of all proposed polymorphic modes; and third, we develop a unified resource management model versatile enough for diverse INC scenarios. Extensive validation -- spanning model checking, packet/flow simulations, VM emulation, Tofino Testbed, and FPGA/RTL verification -- confirms EPIC's correctness, performance gain, and feasibility.

</details>


### [54] [PopPy: Opportunistically Exploiting Parallelism in Python Compound AI Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18697)
*Stephen Mell,David Mell,Konstantinos Kallas,Steve Zdancewic,Osbert Bastani*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: PopPy是一个系统，通过结合编译时和运行时技术，在调用外部重型组件（如ML模型）的Python应用中自动发现并行化机会，在复合AI应用中实现高达6.4倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复合AI应用的端到端延迟成为关键瓶颈，其执行时间主要由外部组件（如ML模型调用）主导，传统的语言优化系统（如优化编译器）无法处理这些外部组件的开销。

Method: PopPy结合提前编译器和运行时系统，解决了提取Python并行性的三个关键挑战：语言复杂性、动态分发和变量可变性。支持表达力丰富的Python子集，仅需极少的开发者输入即可发现并行性。

Result: 在真实复合AI应用上，相比标准Python执行，PopPy实现了高达6.4倍的端到端执行时间加速，同时保持了顺序程序语义。

Conclusion: PopPy能够有效挖掘复合AI应用中的并行化机会，显著降低其端到端延迟，是一种可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Compound AI applications, which compose calls to ML models using a general-purpose programming language like Python, are widely used for a variety of user-facing tasks, from software engineering to enterprise automation, making their end-to-end latency a critical bottleneck. In contrast to traditional applications, execution time is dominated by the external components, which cannot be handled by traditional language optimization systems, like optimizing compilers.
  To address this problem, we develop PopPy, a system that can uncover parallelization opportunities in Python applications that invoke these heavy external components, including those used in compound AI applications. PopPy supports a very expressive fragment of Python and requires minimal developer input to uncover parallelism. It combines an ahead-of-time compiler with a runtime, addressing three key challenges in extracting parallelism from Python applications: language complexity, dynamic dispatch, and variable mutation. On a set of real-world compound AI applications, PopPy achieves up to $6.4\times$ speedups in end-to-end execution time compared to standard Python execution while preserving the sequential program semantics.

</details>


### [55] [Mosaic: Towards Efficient Training of Multimodal Models with Spatial Resource Multiplexing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18710)
*Yanbo Wang,Yuxuan Wang,Chen Chen,Chunyu Xue,Yu Feng,Anbang Wu,Quan Chen,Yin Chen,Qizhen Weng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Apollo是一个针对多模态模型（MMs）训练的高效系统，通过时间-空间复用（temporal-spatial multiplexing）方式，让多个MM模块共享GPU并精细控制资源配额，从而提升GPU利用率和训练效率。实验表明最高可实现1.31倍的训练加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MM部署方案以时间复用方式将单个GPU分配给单个模块，导致GPU利用率低，训练效率差。亟需一种新方式让多个模块高效共享GPU资源。

Method: 提出时间-空间复用方案；开发灵活轻量的执行引擎以支持任意资源配额下的MM训练；构建精确的性能模型估算不同分配方案下的模块执行时间；采用高效启发式算法生成高质量的MM部署方案。

Result: 在真实测试环境中，Apollo对主流多模态模型训练实现了最高1.31倍的加速，有效提升了训练效率。

Conclusion: 时间-空间复用是一种比传统时间复用更高效的MM训练方式，Apollo系统证明了其在提升GPU利用率和训练效率方面的有效性。

Abstract: With the wide adoption of Multimodal Models (MMs) in real-world scenarios, it is significant to efficiently train emerging MMs that exhibit increasingly complex module architectures. For MM deployment, existing works allocate a GPU to only one MM module in a temporal-multiplexing manner; this compromises training efficiency because a single module often fails to achieve high GPU utilization. To improve GPU utilization and enable efficient MM training, we propose deploying MMs in a temporal-spatial multiplexing manner, allowing multiple MM modules to colocate on a GPU with well-controlled resource quotas. In this paper, we propose Apollo, an efficient MM training system that applies temporal-spatial multiplexing. We first develop a flexible and lightweight execution engine that supports MM training with arbitrary resource quotas, and then build a comprehensive and accurate performance model to estimate module execution time under different allocation plans. With the performance model, we further adopt effective heuristics to derive high-quality MM deployment plans efficiently. Testbed experiments confirm that Apollo effectively improves the training efficiency of popular MMs, with a training speedup of up to 1.31x.

</details>


### [56] [A Readiness-Driven Runtime for Pipeline-Parallel Training under Runtime Variability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18750)
*Ruitao Liu,Xinyang Tian,Shuo Chen,Tingrui Zhang,Guang Yang,Alan Zhao,Wei Xu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出运行时就绪优先流水线（RRFP），一种由就绪驱动的流水线并行训练运行时系统，通过将调度视为非绑定的提示顺序而非强制执行顺序，结合消息驱动的异步通信和轻量级张量并行协调，解决传统流水线并行中因运行时可变性导致的阶段错位和空闲气泡问题。在基于Megatron的框架上实现，最高使用128 GPU，语言任务最高加速1.77倍，多模态任务最高加速2.77倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流水线并行系统在运行时计算和通信存在可变性的情况下，预提交的调度顺序会导致阶段错位和空闲气泡，降低GPU利用率。

Method: RRFP将调度从必须遵循的执行顺序改为仅作为就绪任务的排序提示，结合消息驱动的异步通信、轻量级张量并行协调和就绪集仲裁机制，实现就绪优先的运行时调度。

Result: 在128 GPU规模下，语言任务最高1.77倍加速，多模态任务最高2.77倍加速，跨框架对比中比最快外部系统最高快1.84倍，同时保持训练正确性。

Conclusion: 就绪驱动的方法能有效解决流水线并行中的运行时可变性导致的效率问题，是一种可行且高效的替代方案。

Abstract: Pipeline parallelism is a key technique for scaling large-model training, but modern workloads exhibit runtime variability in computation and communication. Existing pipeline systems typically consume static, profiled, or adaptively generated schedules as pre-committed execution orders. When realized task readiness diverges from the pre-committed order, stages may wait for not-yet-ready work even though other executable work is available, creating stage misalignment, idle bubbles, and reduced utilization.
  We present Runtime-Readiness-First Pipeline (RRFP), a readiness-driven runtime for pipeline-parallel training. RRFP changes how schedules are consumed at runtime: instead of treating a schedule as a sequence that stages must wait to follow, it treats the schedule as a non-binding hint order for ranking currently ready work. To support this model, RRFP combines message-driven asynchronous communication, lightweight tensor-parallel coordination for collective consistency, and ready-set arbitration for low-overhead dispatch.
  We implement RRFP in a Megatron-based training framework and evaluate it on language-only and multimodal workloads at up to 128 GPUs. RRFP improves over fixed-order pipeline baselines across all settings. Using the BFW hint, RRFP achieves up to 1.77$\times$ speedup on language-only workloads and up to 2.77$\times$ on multimodal workloads. In cross-framework comparisons, RRFP with the default BF hint outperforms the faster available external system by up to 1.84$\times$ while preserving training correctness.

</details>

### [57] [Systematic Optimization of Real-Time Diffusion Model Inference on Apple M3 Ultra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16259)
*Yoichi Ochiai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统性地在Apple M3 Ultra上对扩散模型进行优化实验，通过CoreML转换、蒸馏模型SDXS-512和3线程相机流水线组合，实现了512x512分辨率下22.7 FPS的实时相机img2img转换，并揭示了Apple Silicon统一内存架构与CUDA平台截然不同的优化特点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管扩散模型在NVIDIA GPU上的实时图像生成技术发展迅速，但在Apple Silicon等非CUDA平台上的系统优化研究极为有限，亟需填补这一空白。

Method: 在Apple M3 Ultra（60核GPU、512GB统一内存）上进行了10个阶段的综合优化实验，探索了CoreML转换、量化、Token Merging、Neural Engine利用、紧凑模型探索、帧插值、kNN搜索合成、pix2pix-turbo、光流跳帧和知识蒸馏等多种技术，并定量评估每种方法的效果。

Result: 结合蒸馏专用模型SDXS-512的CoreML转换与3线程相机流水线，在512x512分辨率下实现了22.7 FPS的实时相机img2img转换。

Conclusion: CUDA上建立的优化经验在Apple Silicon统一内存架构上并不一定有效，揭示了截然不同的优化图景——量化无加速、并行推理无效、Neural Engine不适合大模型，并为Apple Silicon上的扩散模型推理提供了实用指南。

Abstract: While real-time image generation using diffusion models has advanced rapidly on NVIDIA GPUs, systematic optimization research on non-CUDA platforms such as Apple Silicon remains extremely limited. In this study, we conducted comprehensive optimization experiments across 10 phases targeting the Apple M3 Ultra (60-core GPU, 512 GB unified memory) with the goal of achieving real-time camera img2img transformation. We explored a wide range of techniques including CoreML conversion, quantization, Token Merging, Neural Engine utilization, compact model exploration, frame interpolation, kNN search-based synthesis, pix2pix-turbo, optical flow frame skipping, and knowledge distillation, quantitatively evaluating the effectiveness of each approach. Ultimately, by combining CoreML conversion of the distillation-specialized model SDXS-512 with a 3-thread camera pipeline, we achieved real-time camera img2img transformation at 22.7 FPS at 512x512 resolution. The primary contribution of this work is the systematic demonstration that optimization insights established for CUDA are not necessarily effective on Apple Silicon's unified memory architecture. We reveal an optimization landscape fundamentally different from that of NVIDIA GPUs -- including the absence of speedup from quantization, the ineffectiveness of parallel inference, and the unsuitability of the Neural Engine for large-scale models -- and provide practical guidelines for diffusion model inference on Apple Silicon.

</details>


### [58] [SignMuon: Communication-Efficient Distributed Muon Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16311)
*Neel Mishra,Kushagara Trivedi,Pawan Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Sign-Muon优化器，结合signSGD的多数投票符号聚合与Muon的极坐标框架，实现1比特矩阵感知的分布式训练，大幅降低通信开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模神经网络分布式训练受限于全精度梯度通信和忽略权重矩阵结构的逐坐标优化器，亟需更高效的通信压缩与矩阵感知优化方法。

Method: 每个工作节点通过Newton-Schulz迭代计算动量的极分解得到Muon方向，仅传输元素的符号并通过多数投票聚合；可选局部极分解进一步强制执行正交性且不增加通信成本。

Result: 在CIFAR-10/ResNet-50上330种配置中达到最佳验证准确率92.15%；4-GPU变体在匹配有效批量下减少37%训练时间；nanoGPT上困惑度更低；弱扩展性达16 GPU；通信带宽相比float32减少32倍。

Conclusion: Sign-Muon通过1比特符号通信和矩阵感知优化有效降低了分布式训练通信瓶颈，同时保持良好的收敛性和模型性能。

Abstract: Distributed training of large neural networks is bottlenecked by full-precision gradient communication and by coordinatewise optimizers that ignore the matrix structure of weight tensors. We propose Sign-Muon, a 1-bit, matrix-aware optimizer that combines majority-vote sign aggregation from signSGD with the polar-step framework of Muon. Each worker forms a Muon-style direction by taking the polar factor of its momentum via a Newton--Schulz iteration, transmits only the entrywise signs, and aggregates by majority vote; an optional local polar step further enforces orthogonality at no extra communication cost.
  Under spectral-norm smoothness and bounded-variance stochastic gradients, the spectral-norm normalized sign step yields an $\mathcal{O}(1/\sqrt{T})$ nonconvex rate for an $\ell_1$-based stationarity measure. With unimodal symmetric noise, majority vote across $M$ workers cuts the stochastic term by $1/\sqrt{M}$, matching signSGD. In the $α$-$β$ model, distributed Sign-Muon needs only one integer sum-allreduce per iteration; all orthogonalization is local, giving a $32\times$ bandwidth reduction over float32 ($4\times$ for int8).
  Across 330 CIFAR-10/ResNet-50 configurations Sign-Muon attains the best validation accuracy (92.15\%); its 4-GPU majority-vote variant reaches 92.02\% with 37\% less training time at matched effective batch. On nanoGPT, Sign-Muon achieves lower perplexity and better anytime performance than other sign-based baselines, with favorable weak-scaling up to 16 GPUs.

</details>


### [59] [Orth-Dion: Eliminating Geometric Mismatch in Distributed Low-Rank Spectral Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16341)
*Tatsuhiro Nakamori,Laura Gomezjurado Gonzalez,Ganesh Talluri,Ansh Tiwari,Hideyuki Kawashima,Ioannis Mitliagkas,Guillaume Rabusseau,Hiroki Naganuma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出Dion方法（低秩梯度压缩+列归一化）因几何原因收敛慢于全秩谱方法，提出Orth-Dion用QR正交化替代列归一化，在相同通信成本下匹配全秩谱方法的收敛率，实验验证了理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dion方法在低秩梯度压缩场景下收敛速度显著慢于全秩谱方法（如Muon），现有分析未能揭示该差距的根本原因，需要理论解释并设计改进方法。

Method: 通过非欧几里得光滑性框架分析低秩谱几何，证明列归一化不满足双范数约束导致额外$\sqrt{r}$因子；提出Orth-Dion，用QR正交化替代列归一化；采用自洽不动点论证消除误差反馈分析中的有界漂移假设，使用时均收缩技术。

Result: Orth-Dion达到$O(\sqrt{L_r/T})$收敛率，与全秩谱方法一致；在大规模语言模型预训练实验中验证了$\sqrt{r}$标度律，Orth-Dion以Dion的通信成本填补了与Muon的收敛差距。

Conclusion: 低秩梯度压缩中正交化策略（而非简单列归一化）是匹配全秩谱方法收敛率的关键，Orth-Dion在理论和实践上均有效。

Abstract: Low-rank gradient compression reduces communication in distributed training by representing updates with rank-$r$ factors. Dion is a recent method that approximates Muon, a spectral optimizer that orthogonalizes momentum, using one step of power iteration followed by column normalization (rescaling each column of the right factor to unit length). This makes it compatible with fully sharded data parallel training, but it converges more slowly than full-rank spectral methods. We show that this gap is geometric: column normalization does not yield the rank-$r$ polar factor that Muon implicitly targets, so the resulting direction violates the dual-norm constraint of the low-rank spectral geometry, and the rate picks up an extra factor of $\sqrt{r}$ even though the low-rank approximation of the gradient itself is accurate. The same mismatch enters the smoothness term and the error-feedback recursion in the analysis, which has a knock-on effect on empirical performance. We propose Orth-Dion, which replaces column normalization with QR orthogonalization of the right factor. Under non-Euclidean smoothness, with $L_r$ the curvature constant along rank-$r$ directions, Orth-Dion attains rate $O(\sqrt{L_r/T})$, matching exact spectral methods at the same per-step communication cost as Dion. The proof removes the bounded-drift assumption common in prior error-feedback analyses via a self-consistent fixed-point argument, and uses a time-averaged contraction that only requires the error sequence to contract on average rather than at every step. Experiments on large-scale language model pre-training validate the predicted $\sqrt{r}$ scaling and show that Orth-Dion closes the convergence gap to Muon at Dion's communication cost.

</details>


### [60] [LoopQ: Quantization for Recursive Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16343)
*Rui Fang,Hsi-Wen Chen,Ming-Syan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LoopQ是一种针对循环语言模型（LoopLM）的量化感知后训练量化框架，通过激活缩放、选择性变换、跨循环状态对齐和轨迹感知优化，在W4A4量化下相比最强静态PTQ基线提升68.8%平均准确率并降低87.7%困惑度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环语言模型通过递归重用Transformer块提升参数效率，但参数重用使其在后训练量化中更加脆弱，现有PTQ方法未考虑LoopLM的特殊挑战。

Method: 提出LoopQ框架，包含四个组件：激活缩放（减少跨角色分布偏移）、选择性变换（保留关键路径精度）、跨循环状态对齐（缓解循环间状态复用问题）、轨迹感知优化（减少递归误差累积）。

Result: 在7个基准上，W4A4量化下平均下游准确率提升68.8%，平均困惑度降低87.7%，优于最强静态PTQ基线。

Conclusion: LoopQ有效解决了LoopLM在PTQ中的三个核心挑战，为循环架构的高效部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Looped language models (LoopLMs) improve parameter efficiency by recursively reusing Transformer blocks, enabling deeper computation under a fixed model size. However, this reuse makes LoopLMs more fragile under post-training quantization (PTQ). We present the first systematic study of quantization in LoopLMs and identify three challenges: distribution shift across roles, state reuse across loop transitions, and recursive error accumulation. To address these challenges, we propose LoopQ, a loop-aware PTQ framework that preserves a shared quantized backbone while introducing lightweight adaptations. LoopQ combines activation scaling, selective transformation, cross-loop state alignment, and trajectory-aware optimization to reduce distributional mismatch within loops and error accumulation across loops. Experiments across seven benchmarks show that, under W4A4 quantization, LoopQ improves average downstream accuracy by 68.8% and reduces average perplexity by 87.7% compared with the strongest static PTQ baseline.

</details>


### [61] [HPC-LLM: Practical Domain Adaptation and Retrieval-Augmented Generation for HPC Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16347)
*Nourin Shahin,Izzat Alsmadi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HPC-LLM，一个基于检索增强和领域适配的助手，通过QLoRA微调Llama 3.1 8B模型，结合HPC领域文档和合成数据，在Slurm调度、MPI执行、GPU利用等HPC工作流中提供支持，在JetStream2基础设施上评估表明其以较低资源消耗达到接近更大模型（如Qwen 2.5 14B）的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究人员在使用HPC集群环境、作业调度器、GPU资源和并行计算框架时面临显著的操作障碍，通用LLM缺乏领域特定的操作知识，无法提供可靠的HPC支持。

Method: 采用检索增强生成（RAG）和QLoRA轻量级领域适配方法，构建包含约9000-24000个HPC训练样本的语料库（来源于大学HPC文档、操作示例和合成指令-答案对），对Llama 3.1 8B进行微调，并在JetStream2基础设施上进行评估。

Result: 适配后的8B模型在性能上接近Qwen 2.5 14B等更大的通用模型，同时显著降低了GPU内存需求和推理延迟。

Conclusion: 领域适配和检索增强可以显著提升LLM在HPC操作支持场景中的实用性和效率，较小的适配模型能在实际部署中达到与大型通用模型相当的效果。

Abstract: Modern scientific research increasingly depends on High-Performance Computing (HPC) infrastructures, yet many researchers face significant operational barriers when interacting with cluster environments, job schedulers, GPU resources, and parallel computing frameworks. General-purpose large language models (LLMs) provide useful coding assistance but often lack the domain-specific operational knowledge required for reliable HPC support. This paper presents HPC-LLM, a retrieval augmented and domain-adapted assistant designed to support common HPC workflows including Slurm scheduling, MPI execution, GPU utilization, filesystem management, and cluster troubleshooting. The proposed framework integrates automated documentation ingestion, dense retrieval, lightweight domain adaptation using QLoRA, and local inference within a modular orchestration pipeline. To support domain adaptation, we construct an HPC-oriented corpus from publicly available university HPC documentation, curated operational examples, and synthetic instruction-answer pairs generated from retrieved HPC content. The resulting dataset contains approximately 9,000 to 24,000 HPC-focused training examples spanning job scheduling, GPU computing, distributed training, storage systems, and cluster administration topics. We fine-tune Llama 3.1 8B using QLoRA and evaluate the resulting model against several open weight baselines under retrieval-augmented settings on JetStream2 infrastructure. Experimental results indicate that the adapted 8B model achieves performance comparable to substantially larger general-purpose models while operating under significantly lower GPU memory requirements and inference latency. In particular, the adapted model approaches the performance of Qwen 2.5 14B while requiring substantially fewer computational resources.

</details>


### [62] [Geometric Asymmetry in MoE Specialization: Functional Decorrelation and Representational Overlap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16349)
*Feilong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过Jacobian-PCA-Grassmann统一框架分析MoE架构中专家特化的几何结构，发现专家功能解耦与表示空间重叠共存，且路由稀疏性是塑造该几何结构的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE架构通过稀疏路由实现可扩展容量，但专家特化的几何结构尚未被充分理解。

Method: 提出Jacobian-PCA-Grassmann统一框架，在函数空间和表示空间分析MoE层；在Mistral、Qwen等预训练MoE Transformer上进行实验，并进行路由稀疏性控制实验。

Result: 专家存在强功能解耦（低交叉专家Jacobian对齐）但其表示占据不同但部分重叠的子空间；top-k路由诱导更尖锐的功能分离和更大的子空间发散，而soft路由导致更纠缠的结构。

Conclusion: MoE层可被视为在共享表示流形上实现局部解耦算子的结构，该工作为理解条件计算提供了通用诊断框架。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures achieve scalable capacity through sparse routing, yet the geometric structure of expert specialization remains poorly understood. We introduce a unified Jacobian-PCA-Grassmann framework for analyzing MoE layers in both function space and representation space. Across pretrained MoE Transformers (Mistral, Qwen), we find a consistent structural asymmetry: experts exhibit strong functional decorrelation (consistently low, near-zero cross-expert Jacobian alignment) while their routed representations occupy distinct but partially overlapping subspaces. This indicates that functional decorrelation and representation overlap coexist rather than coincide in MoE specialization. Controlled routing experiments further indicate that routing sparsity appears to be a key factor shaping this geometry: top-k routing induces sharper functional separation and larger subspace divergence, whereas fully soft routing yields more entangled expert structure. Together, these results suggest a geometric interpretation in which MoE layers may be viewed as implementing locally decorrelated operators over overlapping submanifolds on a shared representation manifold, and provide a general diagnostic framework for studying conditional computation in modern Transformer architectures.

</details>


### [63] [S-Bus: Automatic Read-Set Reconstruction for Multi-Agent LLM State Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17076)
*Sajjad Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: S-Bus 是一个 HTTP 中间件，通过服务端 DeliveryLog 机制实现 Observable-Read Isolation (ORI) 一致性，解决多 LLM Agent 共享可变自然语言状态时的结构性竞态条件 (SRC) 问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多 Agent 框架（如 LangGraph、CrewAI、AutoGen）在共享可变状态上缺乏写所有权语义，导致结构性竞态条件（写-写冲突、跨分片脏读）静默地破坏 Agent 输出。

Method: 提出 S-Bus 中间件，核心是服务端 DeliveryLog（每个 Agent 的 HTTP GET 日志），可在不修改 Agent SDK 且基于 HTTP/1.1 的情况下，在提交时自动重建每个 Agent 的读集。DeliveryLog 提供 Observable-Read Isolation (ORI) 部分因果一致性，防止共享分片协作时的结构性竞态。

Result: 通过 TLAPS 机器验证（ReadSetSoundness 和 ORICommitSafety）、TLC 穷举模型检测（N=3，20,763,484 个状态，零违规）、Dafny 验证 9 个归纳正确性引理；与 PostgreSQL 17 SERIALIZABLE 和 Redis 7 WATCH/MULTI 对比，427,308 个 HTTP-409 冲突中实现零 Type-I 损坏。

Conclusion: ORI 的操作范围是拓扑条件依赖的：在专用分片工作负载中语义中立；在单分片协作写入中因保留传播并发矛盾而有害。

Abstract: Concurrent LLM agents sharing mutable natural-language state produce Structural Race Conditions (SRCs): write-write and cross-shard stale-read conflicts that silently corrupt agent output. Existing multi-agent frameworks (LangGraph, CrewAI, AutoGen) provide no write-ownership semantics over shared state.
  We present S-Bus, an HTTP middleware whose central mechanism is a server-side DeliveryLog: a per-agent log of HTTP GET operations that automatically reconstructs each agent's read set at commit time without agent SDK changes under HTTP/1.1. The consistency property the DeliveryLog provides -- Observable-Read Isolation (ORI), a partial causal consistency over the HTTP-observable projection of the read set -- prevents structural race conditions when agents collaborate via shared shards.
  Three contributions: (C1) The DeliveryLog mechanism for automatic HTTP-traffic-based read-set reconstruction, with three-tier mechanised evidence: ReadSetSoundness and ORICommitSafety machine-checked in TLAPS (modulo one retained typing axiom); exhaustive TLC at N=3 (20,763,484 distinct states, zero violations); Dafny discharges 9 inductive soundness lemmas. (C2) Empirical structural-conflict prevention parity against PostgreSQL 17 SERIALIZABLE and Redis 7 WATCH/MULTI on shared-shard contention sweeps with 427,308 active HTTP-409 conflicts: zero Type-I corruptions across all three backends. (C3) ORI's operating envelope is topology-conditional: semantically neutral in dedicated-shard workloads; harmful in single-shard collaborative writing because preservation propagates concurrent contradictions.
  Source code: https://github.com/sajjadanwar0/sbus

</details>


### [64] [OSCAR: Offline Spectral Covariance-Aware Rotation for 2-bit KV Cache Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17757)
*Zhongzhu Zhou,Donglin Zhuang,Jisen Li,Ziyan Chen,Shuaiwen Leon Song,Ben Athiwaratkun,Xiaoxia Wu*

Main category: cs.LG
