Without any climate supervision, KAN-SAE identifies an interpretable European heatwave feature spatially concentrated over western Europe, and a western Pacific typhoon tracker confirmed by causal steering experiments. Our results demonstrate that nonlinear activations are essential for mechanistic interpretability of deep learning weather prediction models, recovering climate features that remain invisible to linear baselines.

</details>


### [99] [Q-LocalAdam: Memory-Efficient Client-Side Adaptive Optimization for Edge Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17552)
*Vedant Waykole,Haroon R. Lone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Q-LocalAdam，一种针对联邦学习边缘设备的分层感知8比特量化优化器，利用动量和方差的不同统计特性（动量对称有界、方差跨越八个数量级且呈对数正态分布），实现3.37倍优化器内存压缩且无精度损失，在极端数据异构下甚至提升5.74个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在边缘设备上面临非独立同分布数据和严格内存限制的双重挑战。自适应优化器（如Adam）虽能稳定训练但需存储全精度的动量和方差状态，使客户端内存开销增加约三倍，限制了可部署模型大小和并发联邦任务数量。

Method: 提出Q-LocalAdam，对动量使用块状线性编码进行8比特量化（因为其对称有界），对方差使用对数空间编码进行8比特量化（因为其跨越八个数量级且呈对数正态分布），模型参数保持全精度，不修改联邦通信协议。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100数据集上，不同数据异构程度（α∈{0.1,0.5,1.0,IID}）下，Q-LocalAdam实现3.37倍优化器内存减少，中等异构下无精度损失，极端异构下显著提升（CIFAR-100, α=0.1时+5.74个百分点），多种子验证统计显著（p<0.01）。

Conclusion: 分布感知的量化设计对于联邦学习优化器状态压缩至关重要，朴素均匀量化会导致随机性能；Q-LocalAdam可在不修改联邦协议的前提下，在内存受限的边缘设备上支持更大模型和更多并发工作负载。

Abstract: Federated learning on edge devices must cope with non-IID client data and tight memory budgets. Adaptive optimizers like Adam stabilize training under data heterogeneity but require storing full-precision momentum and variance states, often tripling client memory overhead. This limits deployable model sizes and concurrent federated jobs on resource-constrained devices.
  We empirically observe that momentum and variance in federated Adam exhibit fundamentally different statistical properties: momentum values are symmetric and bounded, while variance spans eight orders of magnitude with log-normal structure. Motivated by this asymmetry, we propose \textbf{Q-LocalAdam}, which applies distribution-aware 8-bit quantization block-wise linear encoding for momentum and log-space encoding for variance while keeping model parameters in full precision.
  Across CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100 under varying data heterogeneity ($α\in \{0.1, 0.5, 1.0, \text{IID}\}$), Q-LocalAdam achieves $3.37\times$ optimizer memory reduction with no accuracy loss under moderate heterogeneity and significant improvements under extreme heterogeneity (e.g., +5.74pp on CIFAR-100, $α=0.1$). Multi-seed validation confirms statistical significance ($p<0.01$). In contrast, naive uniform quantization degrades to random performance, demonstrating that distribution-aware design is essential. Q-LocalAdam enables larger models and more concurrent workloads on memory-constrained edge devices without modifying the federated protocol.

</details>


### [100] [Adaptive Generate-Rank-Verify: Inference-Time Search with Costly Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17609)
*Shaddin Dughmi,Mahdi Haghifam,Yusuf Hakan Kalayci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文形式化了推理时语言模型管道中的"生成式主动搜索"问题——使用廉价奖励信号和昂贵验证器组合，提出了ADAP算法，该算法自适应地增加采样响应数和排名靠前的验证数，理论上达到分布感知最优策略的常数倍期望成本，并在数学推理和编程任务上验证了效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多推理时语言模型管道需要组合廉价奖励信号（如奖励模型打分）和昂贵验证器（如精确答案检查或隐藏测试执行），如何在成本约束下高效找到正确样本是一个关键问题，现有方法缺乏理论分析和最优策略刻画。

Method: 从学习理论视角形式化生成式主动搜索为成本敏感的首阳搜索问题，提出ADAP（Adaptive Generate-Rank-Verify）算法：采用shellwise自适应策略，逐步增加采样响应数量和对排名靠前的候选进行验证，并给出了在单调性假设下的理论保证（常数因子最优性）。

Result: 理论上：证明了ADAP在期望成本上达到分布感知最优策略的常数倍；给出了基于中心星数的学习理论下界，说明分数-标签关系的结构性假设是必要的。实验上：在数学推理和竞争性编程任务上，ADAP优于固定非自适应策略和难度自适应基线。

Conclusion: 本文为推理时语言模型管道中的成本敏感搜索提供了理论框架和实用算法，在保证理论最优性的同时实现了实际性能提升。

Abstract: Many inference-time language-model pipelines combine a cheap reward signal with an expensive verifier, such as exact answer checking in mathematical reasoning or hidden-test execution in code generation.
  We formalize this setting using a learning-theoretic lens as generative active search: a cost-sensitive first-positive search problem in which a policy adaptively samples candidates from an unknown distribution, observes cheap scores, and pays for verifier labels until it finds a positive example. For a fixed prompt, the generator and reward model induce two unknown objects: a distribution over reward scores and a score-conditioned success function. When these quantities are known, we characterize the distribution-aware optimal policy using a dynamic programming approach. In the realistic and practical setting where both the score distribution and success function are unknown, we propose ADAP, a shellwise adaptive generate-rank-verify algorithm that progressively increases the number of sampled responses and top-ranked verifications. Under the monotonicity assumption that higher reward scores are no less likely to pass verification, we show that ADAP achieves expected cost within a constant factor of the distribution-aware optimum. We complement this result with learning-theoretic lower bounds, based on a centered star number, showing that structural assumptions on the score--label relationship are necessary. Experiments on mathematical reasoning and competitive programming validate the predicted advantage over both fixed non-adaptive policies and difficulty-adaptive baselines.

</details>


### [101] [LLMForge: Multi-Backend Hardware-Aware Neural Architecture Search with Infinite-Head Attention for Edge Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17653)
*Xinting Jiang,Junyi Luo,Ruichen Qi,Kauna Lei,Ben Laurie,Gregory Kielian,Mehdi Saligane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LLMForge是一个面向边缘设备上亚十亿参数Transformer语言模型的硬件感知神经架构搜索（NAS）框架，通过无限头注意力（IHA）、Forge-Former代理和Forge-DSE多目标优化引擎，在不同硬件（GPU、脉动阵列加速器、环形数据流边缘加速器）上搜索最优架构，并在300M尺度上得到了比SmolLM2-360M和Qwen-0.5B更优的变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备上部署亚十亿参数Transformer语言模型时，内存带宽、能量和热预算的严格限制使得架构选择和加速器特定成本成为高效推理的核心，而现有方法缺乏硬件感知的架构搜索能力。

Method: 提出LLMForge框架，包含三个组件：1) 无限头注意力（IHA）将查询头数、KV组数、每个头的查询/键和值维度解耦，扩展注意力配置空间约400倍；2) Forge-Former编码器代理用于排序架构候选；3) Forge-DSE基于NSGA-II的多目标设计空间探索引擎，结合多后端硬件成本模型（GPU、脉动阵列、环形数据流边缘加速器）。

Result: 在不同硬件上搜索到的架构明显不同，反映了各硬件的成本瓶颈。在环形芯片上得到三个300M级别的帕累托最优变体：准确变体验证损失2.798且参数更少；能量优化变体每token能耗降低40%；延迟优化变体TTFT和TPOT降低43%。

Conclusion: 硬件感知的NAS对于边缘Transformer语言模型部署是必要的，不同硬件会导向不同的最优架构，LLMForge能够有效生成面向特定硬件的部署感知变体。

Abstract: Sub-billion-parameter Transformer language models are increasingly deployed on edge devices, where the privacy, latency, and operating-cost advantages of on-device inference are constrained by tight memory-bandwidth, energy, and thermal budgets that make architectural choice and accelerator-specific cost central to efficient inference. We present LLMForge, a hardware-aware neural architecture search (NAS) framework whose three composable contributions together make edge-LM architecture search hardware-conditioned, since different substrates impose different hardware cost bottlenecks. Infinite-Head Attention (IHA) decouples the number of query heads, KV groups, and per-head query/key and value dimensions, expanding the feasible per-layer attention configuration space by approximately 400x over grouped-query attention within our search-space ranges. Forge-Former, an encoder-based surrogate for ranking architectural candidates, outperforms MLP and random-forest baselines. Forge-DSE, an NSGA-II-based design-space-exploration engine, pairs Forge-Former with a multi-backend hardware cost model spanning GPUs, systolic accelerators, and ring-dataflow edge accelerators. Across four different hardware substrates, the searches converge to visibly different architectures whose shapes track each substrate's cost bottleneck. On the multi-chip ring substrate, our co-search returns three 300M-scale deployment-aware variants on the Pareto front. Each is re-trained on FineWeb-Edu-10BT under matched recipe against SmolLM2-360M and Qwen-0.5B architecture baselines. The accurate variant has the lowest validation loss 2.798 and competitive benchmark performance with fewer parameters, the energy-optimized variant lowers energy per token by 40%, and the latency-optimized variant lowers TTFT and TPOT by 43%.

</details>


### [102] [Learning Variable-Length Tokenization for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17779)
*Minhao Wang,Bowen Wu,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VarLenRec框架，通过可变长度编码解决生成式推荐中流行商品与长尾商品之间的编码长度矛盾。核心包括：基于信息论的流行度加权信息预算分配（PIBA）证明最优ID长度与流行度呈负幂律关系；双曲残差量化利用庞加莱球的指数体积增长实现分层编码容量；软长度控制器通过连续层保留概率实现可微分长度预测。实验表明该方法在推荐精度和训练/推理效率上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐对所有商品使用固定长度语义标识符编码，忽略了不同商品（特别是流行商品与长尾商品）所需编码容量的差异。作者发现"流行度-长度悖论"：流行商品只需短ID即可获得最优性能，而长尾商品需要更长的编码来捕获判别性语义。

Method: 提出VarLenRec框架：(1) 流行度加权信息预算分配（PIBA），从信息论角度证明最优ID长度应与流行度成负幂律关系；(2) 双曲残差量化（HRQ），利用庞加莱球的指数体积增长来自然分层编码容量，解决欧氏空间无法支持可变码长的问题；(3) 软长度控制器（Soft Length Controller），通过连续层保留概率实现可微分长度预测，由PIBA导出的先验进行正则化。

Result: 在四个数据集上的大量实验表明，VarLenRec在推荐精度和训练/推理效率上显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 揭示了生成式推荐中自适应编码容量的重要性，即不同流行度的商品需要不同长度的标识符以实现最优性能。

Abstract: Generative recommendation reformulates recommendation as next-token prediction over discrete semantic identifiers (IDs). A fundamental yet unexplored design choice is that existing methods employ fixed-length tokenization for all items, implicitly assuming uniform encoding capacity regardless of item characteristics. Through systematic experiments across four datasets, we discover the Popularity-Length Paradox: popular items achieve optimal performance with short IDs, while tail items require substantially longer codes to capture discriminative semantics. This reveals a critical mismatch where popular items benefit from abundant collaborative signals and require minimal semantic detail, whereas tail items must rely on fine-grained content features due to sparse interaction data.
  To address this, we propose VarLenRec, a framework for learning variable-length tokenization. We develop Popularity-Weighted Information Budget Allocation (PIBA), an information-theoretic framework proving that optimal ID length should scale as a negative power of popularity. Directly implementing variable-length allocation faces two technical challenges: standard Euclidean residual quantization lacks geometric capacity to support diverse code lengths without distortion, and discrete length decisions are non-differentiable. We address these through Hyperbolic Residual Quantization, which leverages the exponential volume growth of the Poincaré ball to naturally stratify encoding capacity, and a Soft Length Controller, which enables differentiable length prediction via continuous layer retention probabilities regularized by PIBA-derived priors. Extensive experiments demonstrate that VarLenRec achieves significant improvements over state-of-the-art methods in recommendation accuracy and training/inference efficiency, revealing the importance of adaptive encoding capacity in generative recommendation.

</details>


### [103] [AMO: Adaptive Muon Orthogonalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17806)
*Xinlin Zhuang,Panyi Ouyang,Yichen Li,Jiangming Shi,Yizhang Chen,Shuman Liu,Ying Qian,Weiyang Liu,Haibo Zhang,Imran Razzak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应Muon正交化方法(AMO)，通过观察训练初期各参数矩阵的几何特性来动态分配Newton-Schulz迭代预算，解决现有Muon变体统一调度导致的正交化质量不均问题，在Llama3.1-1.4B和Qwen3-1.7B上分别提升+0.76和+0.51的平均下游性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Muon优化器变体对所有参数矩阵采用统一的Newton-Schulz调度策略，忽略了不同矩阵在正交化难度上的差异及其对性能的影响。作者通过实证研究发现这种跨矩阵的异质性普遍存在，且主要由矩阵几何特性决定。

Method: 提出AMO(Adaptive Muon Orthogonalization)，一种"先观察后执行"的方法：在训练早期按算子类型测量权重几何特性，然后利用这些信号为后续训练分配NS预算。

Result: 在标准预训练、延长预训练和持续预训练设置下，AMO均优于统一调度的Muon基线，在Llama3.1-1.4B上提升+0.76，在Qwen3-1.7B上提升+0.51（12个评估任务的平均下游性能）。

Conclusion: 自适应分配正交化预算能有效提升大模型预训练效果，验证了考虑矩阵几何异质性对优化器设计的重要性。

Abstract: Muon has recently emerged as a competitive alternative to AdamW for large-scale pre-training, with orthogonalization via Newton-Schulz (NS) iterations as its core operation. Existing Muon variants apply a uniform NS schedule to all parameter matrices, overlooking possible differences in orthogonalization difficulty and its impact on performance. Through a systematic empirical study, we show that this per-matrix heterogeneity is pervasive and largely determined by matrix geometry, which evolves dynamically across operator types, training stages, and network depths. As a result, uniform NS schedules can lead to uneven orthogonalization quality across the model. Motivated by these findings, we propose Adaptive Muon Orthogonalization (AMO), an observe-then-commit method that measures weight geometry by operator type early in training and then uses these signals to allocate the NS budget for the remainder of training. AMO delivers consistent improvements over uniform-schedule Muon across standard, prolonged, and continual pre-training, surpassing the strongest baseline by +0.76 on Llama3.1-1.4B and +0.51 on Qwen3-1.7B in average downstream performance of 12 evaluation tasks.

</details>


### [104] [Agentic Cost-Aware Query Planning with Knowledge Distillation for Big Data Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17831)
*Mahdi Naser-Moghadasi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合规则教师规划器、UCB1 bandit探索、成本感知预测和知识蒸馏的轻量级学生规划器的代理查询规划系统，用于资源受限环境下的SQL查询优化，实现23%延迟降低和15倍更快推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大数据分析中的查询优化计算成本高，传统优化器在资源受限环境下无法满足内存和延迟约束。

Method: 使用规则教师规划器生成SQL计划，UCB1 bandit搜索在资源约束下探索计划空间，Random Forest成本模型预测查询延迟，通过知识蒸馏将教师-bandit决策迁移到轻量级学生规划器（Logistic Regression或Gradient Boosting）。

Result: 在NYC Taxi和IMDB数据集上，与默认规划器相比实现23%延迟降低，94%约束满足率；学生规划器以89%准确率复现最优计划，推理速度提升15倍。

Conclusion: 所提系统在资源受限环境下有效降低查询延迟，且单文件实现可复现，适用于资源有限机器的数据分析。

Abstract: Query optimization in big data analytics remains computationally expensive, particularly for resource-constrained environments where traditional optimizers fail to satisfy memory and latency constraints. We present an agentic query planning system that combines a rule-based teacher planner, UCB1 bandit exploration, cost-aware prediction, and knowledge distillation to a lightweight student planner. Our teacher planner generates SQL plans using six key optimization strategies, while UCB1 bandit search efficiently explores the plan space under explicit resource constraints. A Random Forest cost model predicts query latency from plan features, enabling cost-aware decisions. A distilled student planner (Logistic Regression or Gradient Boosting) learns to mimic teacher-bandit decisions for fast inference. Evaluation on NYC Taxi and IMDB datasets demonstrates 23% latency reduction compared to default planners while maintaining 94% constraint satisfaction. The student planner achieves 89% accuracy in replicating optimal plans with 15x faster inference time. Our single-file implementation enables reproducible big-data analytics on resource-limited machines and is publicly available at https://github.com/mahdinaser/agentic-kd-planner.

</details>


### [105] [SNLP: Layer-Parallel Inference via Structured Newton Corrections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17842)
*Ligong Han,Kai Xu,Hao Wang,Akash Srivastava*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Structured Newton Layer Parallelism (SNLP)框架，通过结构化牛顿迭代方法打破Transformer层间顺序依赖，实现层并行推理。在0.5B Nanochat模型上实现2.3倍加速同时PPL降低6.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Transformer推理中逐层顺序执行形成延迟瓶颈，常规张量/流水线并行无法消除层间依赖。作者希望利用非线性残差方程视角，通过牛顿迭代实现层并行计算，突破推理延迟限制。

Method: 将隐状态跨层轨迹视为非线性残差方程的解，用并行牛顿风格迭代近似。提出SNLP框架：在残差Transformer中采用Identity Newton (IDN)将校正简化为前缀和更新；在mHC架构中采用HC Newton (HCN)利用残差混合矩阵；并引入SNLP-aware正则化训练模型适应少量牛顿迭代。

Result: SNLP正则化降低基线PPL 4.7%-23.4%；在0.5B Nanochat模型上实现2.3倍加速并改善PPL 6.1%。现成预训练模型兼容性较差，精确收敛会恢复顺序计算而非提供单调推理扩展。

Conclusion: 层并行推理不仅是对顺序执行的数值近似，还可作为有用的求解器诱导推理偏置。SNLP方法在训练感知正则化下能同时提升速度和模型质量。

Abstract: Autoregressive language models execute Transformer layers sequentially, creating a latency bottleneck that is not removed by conventional tensor or pipeline parallelism. We study whether this layerwise dependency can be relaxed by treating the hidden-state trace across layers as the solution of a nonlinear residual equation and solving it with parallel Newton-style updates. While this view is principled, exact Newton corrections require expensive Jacobian-vector products and naive fixed-point iterations are unstable on trained Transformers. We introduce Structured Newton Layer Parallelism (SNLP), a training and inference framework that replaces exact layer Jacobians with cheap architecture-induced surrogate dynamics. In residual Transformers, this yields Identity Newton (IDN), where the correction reduces to a prefix-sum-like update; in mHC-style architectures, HC Newton (HCN) uses the model's residual mixing matrix. We further introduce SNLP-aware regularization, which trains models to make one or a few structured Newton iterations accurately approximate the sequential forward. Experiments on nanochat-scale Transformers show that SNLP regularization improves layer-parallel compatibility and can also improve standard sequential perplexity, reducing baseline PPL by 4.7%-23.4%. At inference time, SNLP combined with layer fusion and chunkwise decomposition achieves practical wall-clock speedups: on a 0.5B Nanochat model, it reaches 2.3x speedup while still improving PPL by 6.1%. These results suggest that layer-parallel inference is not merely a numerical approximation to sequential execution, but can act as a useful solver-induced inference bias. We also characterize limitations: off-the-shelf pretrained models are less amenable to this procedure, and exact convergence recovers the sequential computation rather than providing monotonic inference-time scaling.

</details>


### [106] [$\boldsymbol{f}$-OPD: Stabilizing Long-Horizon On-Policy Distillation with Freshness-Aware Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17862)
*Xianwei Chen,Shimin Zhang,Jibin Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出f-OPD框架，通过样本级新鲜度评分自适应调控异步策略蒸馏中的样本影响，在保持同步优化性能的同时保留异步执行的高吞吐优势，适用于长程agentic后训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模语言模型的在策略蒸馏（OPD）中，异步执行虽提升系统效率，但会偏离理想的在策略目标，导致性能下降，亟需解决这一性能-效率权衡问题。

Method: 理论上将目标偏差分解为rollout漂移和监督漂移，提出样本级新鲜度评分量化缓冲样本的可靠性；基于该信号设计f-OPD框架，自适应调节陈旧样本影响并约束异步训练下的策略漂移。

Result: 在推理、工具使用和编码agent任务中，f-OPD在性能上与同步优化相当，同时大幅保留了异步执行的高吞吐优势。

Conclusion: 首次建立了OPD中性能-效率权衡的有效方案，为长程agentic后训练规模化奠定了基础。

Abstract: Scaling on-policy distillation (OPD) for large language models (LLMs) confronts a fundamental tension: asynchronous execution is necessary for system efficiency, but structurally deviates from the ideal on-policy objective. To address this challenge, we theoretically decompose the objective discrepancy into rollout drift and supervision drift, capturing staleness in student rollout and teacher context, respectively. Building on this, we introduce a sample-level freshness score that quantifies the reliability of a buffered sample with respect to the on-policy objective. Guided by this signal, we further propose f-OPD, a novel framework that adaptively regulates stale-sample influence and constrains policy drift accumulated under asynchronous training. Across reasoning, tool-use, and coding-agent tasks of increasing interaction horizon, f-OPD consistently achieves task performance comparable to synchronous optimization while largely retaining the throughput advantages of asynchronous execution. Our results establish the first recipe for achieving a performance-efficiency trade-off in OPD, paving the way for long-horizon agentic post-training at scale.

</details>


### [107] [CoX-MoE: Coalesced Expert Execution for High-Throughput MoE Inference with AMX-Enabled CPU-GPU Co-Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17889)
*Mu-Young Son,Yi Chen,Seungjae Yoo,Soongyu Choi Joo-Young Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoX-MoE提出了一种AMX加速的CPU-GPU协同系统，通过合并专家执行和选择性注意力卸载来优化混合专家模型推理吞吐量，相比FlexGen和MoE-Lightning分别实现最高7.1倍和2.4倍吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE架构在吞吐导向推理中面临GPU显存压力，现有专家卸载方案存在工作负载碎片化导致内存受限，以及CPU卸载受限于PCIe传输速率和注意力计算适用性不足等问题，限制了端到端吞吐量。

Method: 提出CoX-MoE系统：(i) 合并感知编排策略，采用普通批次而非微批次进行专家计算和选择性注意力卸载；(ii) 静态专家感知分层方案，预分配高频激活专家至GPU，减少PCIe传输开销并平衡CPU与GPU负载。

Result: 相比FlexGen和MoE-Lightning，CoX-MoE分别实现最高7.1倍和2.4倍的吞吐量提升。

Conclusion: AMX使能的CPU-GPU协同策略能有效缓解MoE推理中的显存压力和PCIe瓶颈，显著提升系统吞吐量。

Abstract: The Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architecture improves computational efficiency via sparse expert activation, but throughput-oriented inference faces substantial GPU memory pressure due to a significant parameter size and intermediate data. Prior works attempt to mitigate this using expert offloading with micro-batching or by offloading computation to the CPU. However, the fragmented workload resulting from micro-batching degrades operational intensity, causing expert execution to become memory-bound. Meanwhile, CPU offloading is constrained by slow PCIe transfers and its limited applicability to attention computation in the decode stage. Consequently, these inefficiencies prevent effective system utilization, severely restricting the end-to-end throughput of MoE inference.
  To address these challenges, this paper proposes CoX-MoE, an Advanced Matrix Extensions (AMX)-enabled CPU-GPU collaborative system that comprehensively optimizes MoE inference by combining coalesced expert execution with strategic workload orchestration for higher throughput. CoX-MoE introduces (i) a coalescing-aware orchestration policy to jointly optimize resource allocation by adopting ordinary batch, instead of micro-batch, for expert computation and selective attention offloading, and (ii) a static expert-aware stratification scheme that pre-assigns frequently activated experts to the GPU, mitigating PCIe transfer overhead and balancing workload for the CPU and GPU during inference. Compared to state-of-the-art frameworks, CoX-MoE delivers significant gains, achieving up to 7.1x and 2.4x higher throughput than FlexGen and MoE-Lightning, respectively.

</details>


### [108] [Lightweight Gaussian Process Inference in C++ on Metal and CUDA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17898)
*Yu-Hsueh Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LightGP 是一个无外部依赖的 C++17 高斯过程回归库，提供 Python 绑定，支持 Apple Metal、NVIDIA CUDA 和 CPU 加速路径，在多个规模上相比 GPyTorch 实现 1.5-8.7 倍加速，并可通过 pip 安装。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Python GP 库（GPyTorch、GPflow）基于深度学习框架构建，存在调度开销大、依赖项多的问题，需要一个轻量、高性能的 GP 推理库。

Method: 使用 C++17 编写核心库，通过 pybind11 提供 Python 绑定，支持四种推理路径（精确 Cholesky、无矩阵共轭梯度、稀疏变分自由能、结构化核插值 FFT），后端支持 Metal、CUDA、Accelerate 和 OpenBLAS。

Result: 在 Apple M4 上 CPU 路径比 GPyTorch 快 2.6-8.7 倍（精确 GP）和约 1.5 倍（稀疏 GP）；在 RTX 3060 上 CUDA 路径快 2.3-6.7 倍；Metal 上的无矩阵核向量积在 N=20,000 时实现 32 倍加速；FFT 加速 SKI 在 N=200,000 时达到亚毫秒级。

Conclusion: LightGP 证明了通过 C++ 原生实现可以显著降低 GP 推理的开销和依赖项，同时保持 Python 生态的易用性，为高性能 GP 计算提供了一个轻量级替代方案。

Abstract: Gaussian process (GP) inference in Python is dominated by libraries such as GPyTorch and GPflow, which are built on deep-learning frameworks and inherit their dispatch overhead and dependency footprint. We present LightGP, a dependency-free C++17 library for GP regression with Python bindings, supporting Apple Metal and NVIDIA CUDA backends alongside tuned CPU paths via Apple Accelerate and OpenBLAS. LightGP provides four inference paths -- exact Cholesky, matrix-free conjugate gradients, sparse variational free energy, and structured kernel interpolation with FFT -- covering problems from $N{=}100$ to $N{=}500{,}000$. On an Apple M4, LightGP CPU is 2.6--8.7$\times$ faster than GPyTorch CPU for exact GP and ${\sim}1.5\times$ faster for sparse GP at every scale tested. On an NVIDIA RTX~3060, LightGP CUDA is 2.3--6.7$\times$ faster than GPyTorch CUDA for exact GP up to $N{=}2{,}048$, with GPyTorch closing the gap at $N{=}4{,}096$. A fused matrix-free kernel-vector product on Metal achieves 32$\times$ over the explicit path at $N{=}20{,}000$ with $O(N)$ memory, and an FFT-accelerated SKI matvec via Accelerate vDSP runs in sub-millisecond time at $N{=}200{,}000$. LightGP compiles as a single static library with zero external dependencies and is installable via \texttt{pip install lightgp

</details>


### [109] [SAFE-SVD: Sensitivity-Aware Fidelity-Enforcing SVD for Physics Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17985)
*Chengjie Hong,Feixiang He,Yiheng Zeng,Lulu Kang,He Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向物理基础模型(PFM)的压缩方法，通过敏感性感知的保真度强制执行框架，在压缩时显式建模输出函数空间中的损失感知层敏感性，从而在保持物理保真度的同时实现高压缩比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理基础模型(PFM)在AI for Science中日益重要，但模型压缩对于减少内存使用和加速推理至关重要。现有压缩方法忽略物理数据的函数性质（偏导数编码时空动力学且对压缩高度敏感），导致性能严重下降或失败，因此需要一种能保持物理保真度的压缩方法。

Method: 提出敏感性感知的保真度强制执行压缩框架，在输出函数空间中显式建模损失感知的层敏感性，从而在压缩过程中保留对物理保真度关键的结构信息。

Result: 在多个模型和数据集上的实验表明，该方法在保持精度的同时实现了显著更高的压缩比，在某些情况下提升达数个数量级。

Conclusion: 该工作可能引领AI for Science中高效、可部署、可持续的科学基础模型这一新子领域。

Abstract: We propose a new method for compressing physics foundation models (PFMs) which is a new trend in AI for Science. While model compression is essential for reducing memory use and accelerating inference in large foundation models, it remains under-explored for PFMs, where preserving physical fidelity is crucial. The challenge lies in the functional nature of physics data, where partial derivatives encode spatiotemporal dynamics and exhibit high sensitivity to compression. Conventional compression methods ignore this structure, often causing severe performance degradation or failure. To address this, we introduce a sensitivity-aware fidelity-enforcing compression framework that explicitly models loss-aware layer sensitivity in the output function space during compression. This provides a new route to compressing scientific foundation models while preserving accuracy and physical fidelity. Experiments show substantial gains over existing methods across multiple models and datasets, achieving significantly higher compression ratios while maintaining accuracy, in some cases by orders of magnitude. More broadly, the work potentially leads to a new subfield of efficient, deployable, and sustainable scientific foundation models in AI for Science.

</details>


### [110] [Elastic-dLLM: Position Preserving Context Compression and Augmentation of Diffusion LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18165)
*Junyi Wu,Tianchen Zhao,Shaoqiu Zhang,Linfeng Zhang,Guohao Dai,Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出位置保持的[MASK]标记压缩和终端感知增强方法，通过减少dLLM中冗余的[MASK]标记计算来加速解码，同时保持结构信息，并支持长上下文扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去噪式大语言模型(dLLM)虽然支持并行解码，但大量[MASK]标记的重复计算导致计算成本高昂，存在显著的计算冗余。

Method: 通过系统分析验证[MASK]标记的冗余性及其提供结构信息的关键作用，提出位置保持的[MASK]标记压缩和终端感知增强两种技术。

Result: 加速了解码过程，在LLaDA-8B-Instruct和LLaDA-1.5上实现了类似上下文折叠的长上下文扩展；在LLaDA2.0-mini上通过终端[MASK]标记增强生成质量。

Conclusion: 通过压缩冗余[MASK]计算，dLLM可以在保持生成质量的同时显著提升推理效率，并自然扩展到长上下文场景。

Abstract: Unlike autoregressive models, which generate one token at a time, dLLMs denoise a chunk of [MASK] tokens jointly and sample one or more tokens per step; despite enabling parallel decoding, this process incurs substantial computational cost due to the large chunk size of masked tokens. We observe that much of this cost is spent on repeatedly processing the preceding context and many [MASK] tokens with the same feature representations, indicating considerable computational redundancy. In this work, we revisit dLLM's redundancy from the perspective of [MASK] tokens. Through systematic analysis, we verify the redundancy of [MASK] tokens while revealing their critical role in providing structural information. Guided by these findings, we propose position-preserving [MASK] token compression and terminal-aware augmentation. By compressing redundant [MASK] computation, this approach accelerates decoding and further provides a natural extension toward context-folding-like long-context scaling under limited input-length constraints for full-sequence dLLMs such as LLaDA-8B-Instruct and LLaDA-1.5. Moreover, for block dLLMs such as LLaDA2.0-mini, it augments the context with a protected terminal [MASK] token to enhance generation quality with negligible overhead.

</details>


### [111] [Dual-Rate Diffusion: Accelerating diffusion models with an interleaved heavy-light network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18190)
*Grigory Bartosh,David Ruhe,Emiel Hoogeboom,Jonathan Heek,Thomas Mensink,Tim Salimans*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出双速率扩散（Dual-Rate Diffusion），通过稀疏执行高容量上下文编码器并让轻量去噪模型复用其特征，实现2-4倍推理加速而不损失生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型推理时由于反复评估重型神经网络导致计算成本高昂，需要加速方法。

Method: 将模型拆分为重型上下文编码器（稀疏执行提取高维特征）和轻型去噪模型（每步复用特征进行高效采样），两者以不同速率交错执行。

Result: 在ImageNet基准上，计算成本降低2-4倍，且生成质量与标准基线相当。

Conclusion: 双速率扩散能有效加速扩散模型推理，且与蒸馏技术兼容，可进一步在少步生成中提升效率。

Abstract: Diffusion models achieve state-of-the-art generative performance but suffer from high computational costs during inference due to the repeated evaluation of a heavy neural network. In this work, we propose Dual-Rate Diffusion, a method to accelerate sampling by interleaving the execution of a heavy high-capacity context encoder and a light efficient denoising model. The context encoder is evaluated sparsely to extract high-dimensional features, which are effectively reused by the light denoising model at every step to refine the sample efficiently. This approach significantly accelerates inference without compromising sample quality. On ImageNet benchmarks, Dual-Rate Diffusion matches the performance of standard baselines while reducing computational cost by a factor of $2$-$4$. Furthermore, we demonstrate that our method is compatible with distillation techniques, such as Moment Matching Distillation, enabling further efficiency gains in few-step generation.

</details>


### [112] [Prune, Update and Trim: Robust Structured Pruning for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18331)
*Diego Coello de Portugal Mecke,Tom Hanika,Lars Schmidth-Thieme*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Putri是一种针对LLM的结构化后训练剪枝方法，通过更新未剪枝权重补偿剪枝误差、顺序剪枝FFN层以及移除单个注意力头而非完整层，实现了极端稀疏率下的SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理成本高昂，尤其在长上下文推理或资源受限设备上，现有后训练剪枝方法存在不足，需要更有效的剪枝技术来降低模型资源需求。

Method: 提出Putri方法，包含三个改进：1) 更新FFN未剪枝权重以补偿剪枝误差；2) 顺序剪枝FFN层并考虑前一层更新；3) 移除单个注意力头而非完整注意力层，并扩展到分组查询注意力。

Result: 在多个模型、不同稀疏度范围和数据集上的剪枝实验验证了Putri的通用性，尤其能在极端稀疏率下有效剪枝LLM，达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: Putri是一种简单但有效的结构化剪枝方法，在保持简洁性的同时实现了最先进的剪枝性能，能显著降低LLM推理成本。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have experienced significant growth and development in recent years. However, performing inference on LLMs remains costly, especially for long-context inference or in resource-constrained devices. This motivates the development of new post-training pruning (PTP) methods. These methods reduce LLMs' requirements by removing a substantial part of the model's parameters. The discarded weights are selected depending on their impact on the models performance. Current PTP methods prune the models by removing the less informative hidden nodes from the FFN layers, and the least important attention layers. We propose Putri, a PTP method that introduces three changes to the State- of-the-art. First, we update the un-pruned weights of the FFN to compensate for the introduced pruning error. Second, the FFN layers are pruned sequentially, taking into account the updates done to the previous layers. Third, instead of removing full attention layers, we remove individual attention-heads. We extend this method such that it can also address Grouped-Query Attention. In summary, Putri is a structure pruning method which remains simple while showing SOTA performance. Pruning experiments on multiple models with a wide variety of sparsity ranges and on different datasets, validate the generality of Putri. Notably, we demonstrate that, unlike previous methods, Putri can prune LLMs on extreme sparsity ratios. The code is available at: https://github.com/Coello-dev/Putri.

</details>


### [113] [Beyond Inference-Time Search: Reinforcement Learning Synthesizes Reusable Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18374)
*Soheyl Massoudi,Gabriel Apaza,Milad Habibi,Mark Fuge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究能否通过强化学习（GRPO）将组合优化问题的推理成本转移到代码LLM的权重中，使模型学会合成可复用的求解器。在Synergistic Dependency Selection问题上，微调后的Qwen2.5-Coder-14B-Instruct收敛到约束感知的模拟退火模板，达到5.0%的最优解差距，且后生成执行成本比Best-of-64低91倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM处理组合优化问题通常每个实例独立推理（采样/搜索/重复提示），成本高。作者希望强化学习将部分推理成本转移到模型权重中，让模型学会为整个问题族生成可复用的求解器。

Method: 使用GRPO对Qwen2.5-Coder-14B-Instruct进行微调，采用可行性门控奖励（feasibility-gated reward）和轻量结构脚手架，训练模型生成约束感知的模拟退火求解器代码。

Result: 微调后模型99.8%的可行输出收敛到约束感知模拟退火模板，达到5.0%的最优解差距（VBS），后生成执行成本比Best-of-64低91倍。编译一次后的求解器在测试集上保持竞争力，在Job Shop Scheduling上也有迁移证据。

Conclusion: 强化学习可以将组合优化问题的推理成本转移到LLM权重中，但效果对奖励归一化、领域特定设计选择敏感，标准稳定器会降低性能，软可行性门控会失败。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) typically approach combinatorial optimization as an inference-time procedure, solving each instance separately through sampling, search, or repeated prompting. We ask whether reinforcement learning can instead shift part of this reasoning cost into the weights of a code LLM, so that the model synthesizes a reusable solver for an entire problem family. We study this question on Synergistic Dependency Selection (SDS), a controlled variant of constrained Quadratic Knapsack designed to expose a specific failure mode: local signals and strict feasibility constraints make greedy heuristics attractive but unreliable. Under identical scaffolding, Best-of-64 base-model sampling saturates at an approximately 28.7% gap to the global Virtual Best Solver (VBS); code audits show that the base model often retrieves Simulated Annealing templates but misimplements the Metropolis acceptance rule. We fine-tune Qwen2.5-Coder-14B-Instruct with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) using a feasibility-gated reward and light structural scaffolding. The resulting policy converges to a constraint-aware Simulated Annealing template in 99.8% of feasible SDS outputs, achieves a 5.0% gap to that VBS, and is 91 times cheaper in post-generation execution/search cost than cumulative Best-of-64 evaluation. A compile-once check shows that one best frozen solver per seed remains highly competitive when reused unchanged across the SDS test set, while an additional-domain evaluation on Job Shop Scheduling provides narrower but positive evidence that the scaffold transfers beyond SDS. Negative ablations reveal the limits of this recipe: standard stabilizers degrade performance, a soft feasibility gate fails, and results remain sensitive to reward normalization and domain-specific design choices.

</details>


### [114] [Beyond Square Roots: Explicit Memory-Efficient Factorization for Multi-Epoch Private Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18379)
*Nikita P. Kalinin,Aki Rehn,Joel Daniel Andersson,Antti Honkela,Christoph H. Lampert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出γ-BIFR，一种统一的广义分解方法，用于差分隐私模型训练中的相关噪声机制，在低内存、低带宽场景下显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有带限逆分解方法（如DP-λCGD和BISR）在内存效率与效用之间面临权衡：低带宽时BISR表现差，高带宽时DP-λCGD效用有限，缺乏统一的解决方案。

Method: 提出γ-BIFR，一种统一推广了DP-λCGD和BISR的广义分解方法，通过引入可调参数γ灵活控制噪声缓冲大小与相关矩阵结构，在低带宽下提升RMSE、放大RMSE及私有训练性能。

Result: 在低内存、低带宽场景下，γ-BIFR显著提升了RMSE、放大RMSE和多轮训练中的多参与误差的理论保证，同时保持内存效率。

Conclusion: γ-BIFR有效统一并改进了现有带限逆分解方法，为差分隐私训练中效用-内存权衡提供了更优的实际部署方案。

Abstract: Correlated-noise mechanisms are among the most promising approaches for improving the utility of differentially private model training, but rigorous guarantees require explicit, analyzable factorizations, and practical deployment requires memory efficiency. Recent works have developed banded inverse factorizations, which address both requirements by exploiting a banded structure in the correlation matrix. The bandwidth controls the size of the noise buffer used to correlate noise across iterations, and thus governs the tradeoff between utility and memory cost. Existing factorizations highlight this tradeoff: DP-$λ$CGD achieves high memory efficiency by using only a one-step noise buffer, but this limits its utility gains, while the banded inverse square root (BISR) factorization exploits larger correlation windows and is asymptotically optimal for large bandwidths but performs poorly at low bandwidths. We propose $γ$-BIFR, a unified generalization of both factorizations. In the low-memory, low-bandwidth regime, $γ$-BIFR significantly improves RMSE, amplified RMSE, and private training performance, while yielding tighter theoretical guarantees for multi-participation error in multi-epoch training.

</details>


### [115] [GAMMA: Global Bit Allocation for Mixed-Precision Models under Arbitrary Budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18475)
*Zhangyang Yao,Haiyan Zhao,Haoyu Wang,Tianbo Huang,Lihua Zhang,Xu Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GAMMA是一个与量化器无关的框架，在后训练阶段通过增强拉格朗日约束学习模块级精度偏好，并利用整数规划将偏好映射为满足预算的离散分配。其关键特性是分数复用——一次训练支持任意部署目标，在Llama和Qwen模型（8B-32B）上优于固定精度和搜索混合精度方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM混合精度量化中，自动分配比特面临独特挑战：可学习方法需要量化感知训练（对十亿参数模型不可行）；无训练方法依赖静态代理指标，忽略跨模块交互且需为每个预算重新计算；搜索方法昂贵且无法保证精确预算合规。

Method: 提出GAMMA框架：（1）在纯后训练管道中学习模块级精度偏好；（2）使用增强拉格朗日约束优化教师强制隐状态重建目标；（3）通过整数规划将学习到的偏好投影为精确预算可行的离散分配；（4）分数复用：一次训练编码稳定敏感性排名，仅需重新求解整数规划即可适配不同预算。

Result: 在Llama和Qwen模型（8B-32B）上，GAMMA相比固定精度基线提升最多+12.99 Avg.，相比搜索混合精度方法提升最多+7.00 Avg.；能以2.5位平均精度匹配固定3位精度质量，显著降低内存占用。

Conclusion: GAMMA实现了高效的LLM后训练混合精度量化分配，通过分数复用大幅降低多预算适配成本，在较低内存占用下达到较高质量。

Abstract: Mixed-precision quantization improves the budget--accuracy trade-off for large language models (LLMs) by allocating more bits to sensitive modules. However, automating this allocation at LLM scale faces a unique combination of constraints: learnable approaches require quantization-aware training, which is infeasible for billion-parameter models; training-free alternatives rely on static proxy metrics that miss cross-module interactions and must be recomputed per target budget; and search-based methods are expensive without guaranteeing exact budget compliance. We propose GAMMA, a quantizer-agnostic framework that learns module-wise precision preferences entirely within a post-training pipeline. GAMMA optimizes a teacher-forced hidden-state reconstruction objective under an augmented Lagrangian constraint, and projects the learned preferences into exact budget-feasible discrete assignments via integer programming. A key property is score reuse: because the learned preferences encode a stable sensitivity ranking rather than budget-specific weights, a single training run serves arbitrary deployment targets by re-solving only the integer program, reducing per-budget adaptation from hours to a few minutes. Across Llama and Qwen models (8B--32B), GAMMA outperforms both fixed-precision baselines (up to +12.99 Avg.) and search-based mixed-precision methods (up to +7.00 Avg.), and can match fixed 3-bit quality at 2.5-bit average precision, enabling deployment at substantially smaller memory footprints.

</details>


### [116] [DBES: A Systematic Benchmark and Metric Suite for Evaluating Expert Specialization in Large-Scale MoEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18498)
*Jing Wang,Hongxuan Lu,Jazze Young,Shu Wang,Zhimin Xin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DBES 是一个用于诊断 MoE 模型专家专业化的框架，包含五个理论指标，通过干预实验证明利用这些指标进行领域特定后训练可在仅用 15% 资源的情况下提升 66%-94.48% 的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE 模型中的专家专业化程度难以评估，传统方法将架构负载均衡与功能专业化混为一谈，缺乏系统性诊断工具。

Method: 提出了 DBES 诊断框架，包含多领域基准测试和五个指标（路由专业化、归一化有效秩、领域隔离度、路由刚度评分、N-gram 专业度），并通过干预实验验证指标的可操作性。

Result: Qwen 系列表现出模块化专业化（高领域隔离度），DeepSeek 和 GLM 采用分布式协作；利用 DBES 指导后训练，在仅用 15% 资源下实现 66%-94.48% 的专业领域性能提升。

Conclusion: 专家专业化是诊断维度而非性能保证，但这些指标可转化为具体优化算子，为下一代 MoE 系统的设计和后训练优化提供指导。

Abstract: Expert specialization in Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models remains poorly understood, with traditional evaluations conflating architectural load-balancing with functional specialization. We introduce DBES, a comprehensive diagnostic framework combining a multi-domain benchmark with five theoretically grounded metrics: Routing Specialization, Normalized Effective Rank, Domain Isolation, Routing Stiffness Score, and N-gram Expertise measures.
  Critical findings demonstrate distinct specialization paradigms across models: Qwen-series exhibit modular specialization with high domain isolation, while DeepSeek and GLM employ distributed collaboration. However, we emphasize that specialization is a diagnostic dimension, necessary but not sufficient for downstream performance. Most crucially, interventional evidence validates the actionability of these metrics: by using DBES to identify high-specialization expert paths during domain-specific post-training, we achieved 66% to 94.48% improvement in specialized domains with only 15% of original training resources, demonstrating that these diagnostic tools can be converted into concrete optimization operators. This work provides the first systematic methodology for evaluating expert specialization independently of accuracy metrics, offering crucial insights for the design and post-training optimization of next-generation MoE systems.

</details>


### [117] [Beyond Scaling: Agents Are Heading to the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18535)
*Chunlin Tian,Dongqi Cai,Wanru Zhao,Nicholas D. Lane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文论证个人智能体架构必须从云端走向边缘，因为智能体任务的核心特性——与本地上下文的结构耦合和零延迟执行需求——与云中心设计不兼容。通过"前额叶转向"、"数据地理悖论"和"交互对齐循环"三个结构转变来支撑这一论点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前有用的智能体智能的瓶颈已从将世界知识压缩到单一模型转向执行协调系统，而云中心架构无法满足智能体任务对本地上下文和高实时性的要求。

Method: 通过提出三个结构性转变（前额叶转向、数据地理悖论、交互对齐循环）来论证个人智能体架构必须向边缘迁移的论点。

Result: 提出个人智能体的架构必须走向边缘，因为云中心设计无法满足智能体任务对本地高保真上下文和零延迟执行的核心需求。

Conclusion: 给出关于个人智能体下一部署周期的可证伪预测。

Abstract: The bottleneck of useful agentic intelligence has shifted from compressing world knowledge into a single model to executing a coordinated system. This position paper argues that personal-agent architecture must move to the edge because the core properties of agentic intelligence tasks, particularly their structural coupling with high-fidelity local context and the need for zero-latency execution loops, do not sit well with cloud-centric designs. We develop this claim through three structural shifts. First, the Prefrontal Turn: the main marginal lever of capability has moved from pre-training scale to framework-level executive control. Such control must remain physically close to the environment of action if the agent is to preserve cognitive alignment. Second, the Data-Geography Paradox, the ``dark matter'' of agentic data (local file hierarchies, real-time sensor streams, and transient OS states) degrades, disappears, or loses meaning once prepared for cloud transmission, thereby cutting the agent off from ground-truth context. Third, the interaction-alignment loop, the only economically and ecologically sustainable source of agentic refinement data is the high-fidelity implicit preference signal produced through real-time local interaction. Third, the interaction-alignment loop, the only economically and ecologically sustainable source of agentic refinement data is the high-fidelity implicit preference signal produced through real-time local interaction. We conclude with falsifiable predictions for the next deployment cycle of personal agents.

</details>


### [118] [Pocket Foundation Models: Distilling TFMs into CPU-Ready Gradient-Boosted Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18654)
*Aditya Tanna,Nassim Bouarour,Mohamed Bouadi,Vinay kumar Sankarapu,Pratinav Seth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出通过离线蒸馏将表格基础模型（TFM）的知识转移到XGBoost/CatBoost学生模型中，解决ICL教师泄露标签导致的软标签坍塌问题，采用分层OOF教师标注，在153个分类数据集上实现1.9ms CPU推理（38-860倍加速），达到教师96.5%的AUC性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欺诈评分等场景要求亚毫秒级推理延迟，而现有表格基础模型在GPU上需要151-1275ms，无法满足生产环境实时性要求。作者希望弥合大模型精度与CPU高效推理之间的差距。

Method: 提出离线知识蒸馏框架：使用分层OOF（Stratified Out-of-Fold）教师标注解决ICL教师训练集标签泄露问题，将TabICLv2等表格基础模型的软标签蒸馏到XGBoost/CatBoost树模型学生中，学生模型在CPU上原生运行。

Result: 在153个分类数据集上，TabICLv2蒸馏到XGBoost实现0.882 macro-mean AUC（教师性能的96.5%），推理仅需1.9ms CPU，获得38-860倍加速；显著优于调优后的CatBoost基线（Wilcoxon p=0.0008，胜率51%）。

Conclusion: 离线蒸馏是弥合表格基础模型精度与CPU高效推理的有效途径，但需解决ICL教师特有的标签泄露问题；蒸馏增益集中在低维数据，高维任务中若教师本身不如CatBoost则蒸馏适得其反。

Abstract: A fraud scorer needs to answer in under 2 ms. The best tabular foundation models (TFMs) take 151-1,275 ms on GPU. We close this gap by distilling the TFM offline into an XGBoost or CatBoost student that runs natively on CPU. The central obstacle is specific to in-context learning (ICL) teachers: they leak labels when scoring their own training set, so the soft targets collapse to near-one-hot vectors with no inter-class structure left to distill. Stratified out-of-fold (OOF) teacher labeling prevents this. Across 153 classification datasets drawn from TALENT, OpenML-CC18, TabZilla, and TabArena, distilling TabICLv2 into XGBoost gives 0.882 macro-mean AUC (96.5% of teacher AUC) at 1.9 ms on CPU, a 38x to 860x speedup across teacher-student pairs with a statistically significant edge over a tuned CatBoost baseline (Wilcoxon p = 0.0008; 51% win rate). Four further findings: teacher rank transfers exactly to student rank; gains concentrate on low-dimensional data (< 21 features: +0.011 over CatBoost vs. >21 features: +0.001); multi-teacher averaging helps MLP students (+0.006, p = 0.003) but adds less than 0.001 for tree students; and on high-dimensional tasks where the teacher itself trails CatBoost, distillation makes things worse rather than better. The full pipeline is open-sourced as part of the TabTune library.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [AgentWall: A Runtime Safety Layer for Local AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16265)
*Ashwin Aravind*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentWall是一个针对本地AI agent的运行时安全与可观测层，能在agent操作到达宿主机前进行拦截、策略评估、人工审批和完整审计追踪，实现92.9%的策略执行准确率和亚毫秒级开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI安全研究主要聚焦模型对齐和输入过滤，但未能解决agent意图转化为真实机器操作时的安全问题，尤其在本地环境中agent直接访问文件系统、凭证和基础设施时缺乏运行时控制。

Method: AgentWall作为策略执行的MCP代理和原生OpenClaw插件实现，拦截每个提议的agent动作，基于声明式策略评估，敏感操作需人工审批，并记录完整执行链用于审计和回放，兼容Claude Desktop、Cursor、Windsurf、Claude Code和OpenClaw。

Result: 在14项基准测试中实现了92.9%的策略执行准确率和亚毫秒级开销。

Conclusion: AgentWall为本地AI agent提供了有效的运行时安全保障和可观测能力，填补了agent安全从模型对齐到实际执行之间的空白。

Abstract: The safety of autonomous AI agents is increasingly recognized as a critical open problem. As agents transition from passive text generators to active actors capable of executing shell commands, modifying files, calling APIs, and browsing the web, the consequences of unsafe or adversarially manipulated behavior become immediate and tangible. Existing AI safety work has focused primarily on model alignment and input filtering, but these approaches do not address what happens at the moment an agent's intent becomes a real action on a real machine. This gap is especially acute in local environments, where developers run agents against their own filesystems, credentials, and infrastructure with little runtime control. This paper introduces AgentWall, a runtime safety and observability layer for local AI agents. AgentWall intercepts every proposed agent action before it reaches the host environment, evaluates it against an explicit declarative policy, requires human approval for sensitive operations, and records a complete execution trail for audit and replay. It is implemented as a policy-enforcing MCP proxy and native OpenClaw plugin, working across Claude Desktop, Cursor, Windsurf, Claude Code, and OpenClaw with a single install command. We present the design, architecture, threat model, and policy model of AgentWall, and demonstrate 92.9% policy enforcement accuracy with sub-millisecond overhead across 14 benchmark tests. AgentWall is open-source at https://github.com/agentwall/Agentwall.

</details>


### [120] [ANNEAL: Adapting LLM Agents via Governed Symbolic Patch Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16309)
*Safayat Bin Hakim,Keyan Guo,Wenkai Tan,Alvaro Velasquez,Shouhuai Xu,Houbing Herbert Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ANNEAL是一个神经符号智能体，通过Failure-Driven Knowledge Acquisition机制将重复执行失败转化为对过程知识图的有治理符号编辑，实现持久性结构修复，在跨四个领域的实验中，将重复故障率从72-100%降至0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体虽能从单次执行错误中恢复，但底层过程知识（操作符模式、前置条件、约束）未修复时，会在同一故障上反复失败。现有自演化方法更新prompt、记忆或模型权重，但未直接修复编码任务执行方式的符号结构，且缺乏安全部署所需的治理保证。

Method: ANNEAL采用神经符号架构，核心机制为Failure-Driven Knowledge Acquisition (FDKA)：定位故障操作符，通过受约束的LLM生成合成类型化补丁，经多维评分、符号护栏和金丝雀测试验证后提交，每次编辑保留完整溯源和确定性回滚能力。

Result: 在四个领域27次多种子运行中，ANNEAL是唯一实现持久性结构修复的系统，将重复故障率降至0%（基线系统为72-100%）；消融实验表明移除FDKA后所有结构修复消失，成功率下降高达26.7个百分点。

Conclusion: 有治理的符号修复为持久消除故障提供了权重级和提示级适应之外的互补范式。

Abstract: LLM-based agents can recover from individual execution errors, yet they repeatedly fail on the same fault when the underlying process knowledge--operator schemas, preconditions, and constraints--remains unrepaired. Existing self-evolving approaches address this gap by updating prompts, memory, or model weights, but none directly repair the symbolic structures that encode how tasks are executed, and few provide the governance guarantees required for safe deployment. We introduce ANNEAL, a neuro-symbolic agent that converts recurring failures into governed symbolic edits of a process knowledge graph without modifying foundation model weights. Its core mechanism, Failure-Driven Knowledge Acquisition (FDKA), localizes the responsible operator, synthesizes a typed patch through constrained LLM generation, and validates the proposal via multi-dimensional scoring, symbolic guardrails, and canary testing before commit. Every accepted edit carries full provenance and deterministic rollback capability. Across four domains and 27 multi-seed runs, ANNEAL is the only evaluated system that commits persistent structural repairs--strong baselines such as ReAct and Reflexion achieve high episodic recovery yet retain 72-100% holdout failure rates on recurring faults, whereas ANNEAL reduces these to 0% in the tested recurring-failure settings. Ablation confirms that removing FDKA eliminates all structural repairs and drops success rate by up to 26.7 percentage points. These results suggest that governed symbolic repair offers a complementary paradigm to weight-level and prompt-level adaptation for persistent fault elimination.

</details>


### [121] [From Prompts to Protocols: An AI Agent for Laboratory Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16552)
*Angelos Angelopoulos,James F. Cahoon,Ron Alterovitz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个集成大语言模型与实验室编排的AI代理架构，使科学家能用自然语言交互式创建和监控自动化实验协议，在化学、生物、材料科学三个模拟实验室中实现97%的首轮协议生成成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设置和运行自主实验室需要协调大量仪器和机器人，迫使科学家编写代码、管理配置文件并处理复杂的软件基础设施，降低了科研效率。

Method: 提出AI代理架构，将LLM与实验室编排系统(EOS)集成，采用代理循环机制，包含自动验证和纠错，支持完整的实验生命周期（创建协议、运行监控、结果分析），并提供可视化图形编辑器。

Result: 在化学、生物学、材料科学三个模拟自动化实验室中评估，AI代理首轮协议生成成功率达97%，所需界面操作数量级减少。

Conclusion: AI代理架构能显著降低自动化实验室的软件基础设施门槛，使科学家能够通过自然语言高效地操控复杂实验流程。

Abstract: Automating science laboratories enables faster, safer, more accurate, and more reproducible execution of protocols, accelerating the discovery and testing of new materials, drugs, and more. However, setting up and running autonomous labs requires coordinating numerous instruments and robots, forcing scientists to write code, manage configuration files, and navigate complex software infrastructure. We present an AI agent architecture that integrates large language models with laboratory orchestration, enabling scientists to interactively create and monitor automated lab protocols using natural language. Integrated into the Experiment Orchestration System (EOS), the AI agent operates under an agentic loop with automated validation and error correction, and supports the complete experimental lifecycle: creating protocols, running and monitoring both protocols and closed-loop optimization campaigns, and analyzing results. A visual graph editor renders protocols as interactive node-based diagrams synchronized with the AI agent's protocol representation, enabling seamless alternation between AI-assisted and manual protocol construction. Evaluated on three simulated automated labs spanning chemistry, biology, and materials science, the AI agent achieves a 97% first-attempt protocol generation success rate and an order of magnitude reduction in required interface actions.

</details>


### [122] [Skim: Speculative Execution for Fast and Efficient Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16565)
*Mike Wong,Kevin Hsieh,Suman Nath,Ravi Netravali*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Skim是一个面向Web智能体的投机执行框架，利用目标网站的可预测结构（稳定URL模式、答案格式和任务到轨迹映射），通过离线分析+在线模板匹配+轻量验证器替代大多数步骤的昂贵推理和渲染，将不匹配的请求回退到完整智能体，从而在不损失准确率的情况下降低成本和延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前Web智能体的运行成本高昂，且这种成本并非任务本身的固有属性，而是来自架构设计——每个步骤无论复杂度都依赖前沿模型推理、浏览器渲染和ReAct式规划，导致大量不必要的资源浪费。

Method: 离线分析器捕获网站模式（URL结构、答案格式、任务轨迹映射）；在线阶段将查询匹配到模板，用小模型合成目标URL并提取答案；轻量验证器检查快速路径输出，当检测到错误时回退到完整智能体（以快速路径最终URL热启动以保留轨迹进度）。

Result: 在多个标准Web智能体基准测试上，搭配三种骨干智能体（WebVoyager、AgentOccam、BrowserUse），Skim将每任务中位成本降低1.9倍，延迟降低33.4%，且零准确率损失。

Conclusion: 利用网站的结构化可预测性可以大幅降低Web智能体运行成本，投机执行范式在Web场景中有效，且轻量验证器足以保证可靠性。

Abstract: Skim is a speculative execution framework for web agents that exploits the predictable structure of purpose-built websites. Today's web-agent expense is not intrinsic to the tasks but a property of how agents are composed: frontier-model inference, browser rendering, and ReAct-style planning are applied to every step of every task regardless of complexity. Skim's key observation is that websites enforce stable URL patterns, answer formats, and task-to-trajectory mappings across queries of the same type, so most queries can bypass these heavyweight components entirely. An offline profiler captures these patterns once per site. At runtime, Skim matches each query to a template, synthesizes the destination URL, and extracts the answer with a small model. A lightweight verifier gates each fast-path output against the query and schema; rare misspeculations cascade to the full agent, warm-started by the fast path's final URL to preserve upstream trajectory progress. Across standard web-agent benchmarks paired with three backboneagents (WebVoyager, AgentOccam, BrowserUse), Skim reduces median per-task cost by 1.9x and latency by 33.4% with no accuracy loss.

</details>


### [123] [GRID: Graph Representation of Intelligence Data for Security Text Knowledge Graph Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16714)
*Liangyi Huang,Zichen Liu,Fei Shao,Shang Ma,Mengshi Zhang,Zihao Chen,Yanfang Ye,Xusheng Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出GRID框架，通过构建安全领域监督信号和脚本化任务库，实现端到端的威胁情报知识图谱构建，训练4B小模型在精确率、召回率上取得领先效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM缺乏安全领域知识，且端到端的文档到图谱训练难以通过廉价稳定的奖励信号进行监督，亟需一种高效、可落地的安全文本知识图谱构建方法。

Method: GRID框架：先通过图提取和知识图谱条件文本修订构建可追溯的文章-图谱对齐监督信号；再将文档到图谱学习转化为脚本化任务库（四选多选+三元组级正则匹配），训练基于Qwen3-4B的任务库奖励模型和端到端奖励模型。

Result: 在249篇CTI文章上，任务库奖励模型达到84.62%精确率、64.91%召回率、68.53% Avg F1，优于端到端奖励模型和更弱的基线，且任务库奖励可离线构建并跨后训练复用。

Conclusion: 任务库奖励相比在线端到端LLM评判奖励更稳定高效，为安全领域小模型的知识图谱构建提供了低成本高效果的可行方案。

Abstract: Security knowledge graphs can provide computable external memory for security agents, but constructing them from long-form cyber threat intelligence (CTI) remains difficult: LLMs often lack grounded security-domain knowledge, and end-to-end document-to-graph training is hard to supervise with cheap, stable rewards. We present GRID (Graph Representation of Intelligence Data), an end-to-end framework for security text knowledge graph construction. GRID first builds security-domain supervision from CTI articles by creating traceable article-graph alignments through graph extraction and knowledge-graph-conditioned text revision. It then turns document-to-graph learning into a scripted task bank combining four-option multi-select questions with triple-level regex matching targets, yielding more stable task-specific rewards than repeatedly scoring full graph outputs with an LLM judge. Using this supervision pipeline, we train two Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507-based 4B extractors: a primary Task-bank Reward model and a secondary End2End Reward model with LLM-as-judge precision/recall rewards. On 249 CTI articles from GRID, CASIE, CTINexus, MalKG, and SecureNLP, the Task-bank Reward model with the ontology-guided GRID extraction pipeline reaches 84.62% source-averaged precision, 64.91% source-averaged recall, and 68.53% Avg F1, achieving the best source-averaged recall and near-top Avg F1 with lower token usage and deployment cost. The End2End Reward model reaches 76.91% precision, 53.85% recall, and 58.06% Avg F1. Further analyses show that task-bank rewards can be built once offline and reused across later post-training runs, outperforming online End2End LLM-as-judge reward and weaker alternatives such as Choice-only Reward and End2End SFT without RL.

</details>


### [124] [PopuLoRA: Co-Evolving LLM Populations for Reasoning Self-Play](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16727)
*Roger Creus Castanyer,Geoffrey Bradway,Lorenz Wolf,Maxwill Lin,Augustine N. Mavor-Parker,Matthew James Sargent*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PopuLoRA 是一种基于群体的非对称自博弈强化学习框架，用于 LLM 的后训练。它通过 LoRA 适配器群体中的教师-学生协作进化（交叉变异操作），替代单智能体自博弈中的自校准局限，在代码和数学基准上优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单智能体自博弈训练中模型会自校准到生成自己能稳定解决的简单问题，导致问题空间覆盖不足。作者希望通过群体进化的方式引入竞争关系，促使教师生成更复杂的问题，从而推动学生能力持续提升。

Method: 基于共享冻结基座模型上的 LoRA 适配器群体，划分为教师和学生子群体；教师生成问题，匹配的学生在程序化验证器下求解；通过交叉评估替代自校准；使用 LoRA 权重空间的进化算子（突变和交叉）在群体训练循环中快速生成同秩新成员。

Result: 在 HumanEval+、MBPP+、LiveCodeBench 三个代码基准和 AIME 24/25、AMC 23、MATH-500、Minerva、GSM8K、OlympiadBench 七个数学基准上，群体均值优于计算匹配的单智能体基线；训练奖励虽低，但问题空间覆盖持续扩大。

Conclusion: 群体进化的非对称自博弈框架能有效避免单智能体自校准的局限，通过师生共演化产生更广泛的问题覆盖和更强的泛化性能。

Abstract: We introduce PopuLoRA, a population-based asymmetric self-play framework for reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) post-training of LLMs. Teachers and students are specialised LoRA adapters on a shared frozen base: teachers propose problems, matched students solve them under a programmatic verifier, and cross-evaluation between sub-populations replaces the self-calibration that limits single-agent self-play. A family of LoRA weight-space evolution operators (mutations and crossovers that produce same-rank population members in seconds) serves as the replacement step of a population-based training loop at 7B scale. We instantiate PopuLoRA on top of Absolute Zero Reasoner and compare it against a per-adapter compute-matched single-agent baseline. Where the single agent self-calibrates to generating easy problems it can reliably solve, the population enters a co-evolutionary arms race: teachers produce increasingly complex problems, student solve rates oscillate, and problem-space coverage keeps expanding throughout training. Despite lower training-time reward, the population mean outperforms the baseline on three code benchmarks (HumanEval+, MBPP+, LiveCodeBench) and seven math benchmarks (AIME 24/25, AMC 23, MATH-500, Minerva, GSM8K, OlympiadBench), and even the weakest member of the population beats the baseline on aggregate.

</details>


### [125] [NeuroMAS: Multi-Agent Systems as Neural Networks with Joint Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16757)
*Haoran Lu,Luyang Fang,Wenxuan Zhong,Ping Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了NeuroMAS，一种将多语言智能体系统视为可训练、可扩展的神经网络架构的方法，其中LLM智能体作为节点，中间文本信号作为边，通过强化学习训练实现节点间的通信、特化和协调，将多智能体设计从工作流工程转向架构设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多语言智能体系统通常依赖手工设计的工作流，智能体角色和通信协议需要预先指定，缺乏可训练性和可扩展性。

Method: NeuroMAS将多智能体语言系统建模为类神经网络架构，LLM智能体作为无角色但感知结构的节点，通过强化学习训练自动学习节点间的通信、特化和协调方式，支持深度、宽度、连接性和增长协议等架构维度的扩展。

Result: NeuroMAS在多个基准上显著优于推理时和训练过的多智能体基线；发现组织扩展存在路径依赖性，大的系统从头训练困难，但从小的训练好的系统渐进增长则是可行的。

Conclusion: 学习型神经多智能体系统是LLM的一个有前景的扩展轴，将多智能体设计范式从工作流工程转向架构设计。

Abstract: Multi-agent language systems are often built as hand-designed workflows, where agents are assigned semantic roles and communication protocols are specified in advance. We propose NeuroMAS, a method that first treats a multi-agent language system as a trainable and scalable neural-network-like architecture with LLM agents as nodes and intermediate textual signals as edges. In NeuroMAS, agent nodes are role-free but structure-aware: the topology only determines how information can flow in general, while reinforcement learning training determines how nodes communicate, specialize, and coordinate. This formulation shifts multi-agent design from workflow engineering toward architecture design, where depth, width, connectivity, and growth protocol become scalable sources of capability. Further, we provide a theoretical perspective showing why such modular textual computation is more parameter-efficient when tasks admit hierarchical decompositions. Experiments show that NeuroMAS improves significantly over both inference-time and trained multi-agent baselines. We further find that organizational scaling is path-dependent: larger systems can be challenging to train from scratch, but become feasible when grown progressively from smaller trained systems. These results suggest that learned neural multi-agent systems are a promising scaling axis for LLMs.

</details>


### [126] [Multi-Paradigm Agent Interaction in Practice:A Systematic Analysis of Generator-Evaluator, ReAct Loop,and Adversarial Evaluation in the buddyMe Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16821)
*Xiaohua Wang,Chao Han,Kai Yu,XiaoLiang Xu,Liang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对 buddyMe 开源多模型智能体编程框架中的三种主要智能体交互范式（多智能体编排、ReAct 工具使用循环、记忆增强交互）进行系统分析，形式化了一个五阶段处理流程和六维评估体系，并通过四个真实案例研究得出关键结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 LLM 智能体产生了多种交互范式，但很少有生产系统在统一架构中集成多种范式，缺乏系统性的分析和设计指南。

Method: 形式化五阶段处理流水线（需求预审→任务分解→ReAct执行→实执行验证→对抗性评估讨论），建立六维加权评分评估体系，通过四个真实部署案例（博物馆导览、定时天气任务、综合旅行规划）进行实证研究。

Result: 生成器-评估器预审可检测20%复杂任务的遗漏需求，80%任务通过初始检查；ReAct循环确保稳定子任务执行但导致约30%冗余工具调用；对抗性评估讨论在近70%场景下2-3轮内达成共识，主要用于内容精炼而非逻辑反转。

Conclusion: 提供了构建稳定可靠的多范式智能体系统的实用设计指南，并与 CrewAI、AutoGen、LangGraph、MemGPT 和 A-Mem 进行了跨范式比较。

Abstract: The rapid evolution of Large Language Model (LLM) agents has produced diverse interaction paradigms, yet few production systems integrate multiple paradigms within a unified architecture. This paper presents a systematic analysis of three principal agent interaction paradigms, including Multi-Agent Orchestration (Generator-Evaluator), ReAct Tool-Use Loops, and Memory-Augmented Interaction, as implemented in buddyMe, an open-source multi-model agent programming framework. We formalize a five-stage processing pipeline: Requirement Pre-Review -> Task Decomposition -> ReAct Execution -> Real-Execution Verification -> Adversarial Evaluation Discussion, and establish a six-dimensional evaluation schema with weighted scoring. Through four empirical case studies drawn from real-world deployment logs covering museum guide generation, scheduled weather tasks, and comprehensive tour planning, we draw three key conclusions. First, Generator-Evaluator pre-review detects requirement omissions in 20 percent of complex tasks, with 80 percent tasks passing initial inspection. Second, the ReAct loop ensures stable subtask execution but leads to around 30 percent redundant tool invocations. Third, adversarial Evaluator-Defender discussions reach consensus within 2-3 rounds for nearly 70 percent of scenarios, functioning mainly for content refinement rather than logical reversal. We additionally provide three Mermaid-based architectural diagrams and conduct cross-paradigm comparisons with CrewAI, AutoGen, LangGraph, MemGPT and A-Mem across six system dimensions. The research outcomes offer practical design guidelines for constructing stable and reliable multi-paradigm agent systems.

</details>


### [127] [NGM: A Plug-and-Play Training-Free Memory Module for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16893)
*Yuwen Qu,Wenhui Dong,Chenyang Si,Caifeng Shan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出NGM（N-gram Memory），一个无需训练、即插即用的模块，通过因果N-gram编码器和余弦门控注入器，利用预训练token嵌入构建N-gram表示并注入到上下文表示中，提升模型在代码生成和知识密集型任务上的性能，尤其在Qwen3系列上效果显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有条件记忆模块虽然将知识存储与神经计算解耦，但仍依赖学习到的记忆嵌入，需要额外训练且灵活性受限。

Method: 提出NGM，包含因果N-gram编码器（直接对预训练token嵌入取平均构建N-gram表示）和余弦门控注入器（使用非参数化余弦门控+ReLU将检索到的嵌入调制到上下文表示中），无需单独的记忆表或检索管道。

Result: 在Qwen3系列（0.6B-14B）八个基准上平均提升0.5-1.2点，代码生成任务（LiveCodeBench +3.0）和知识密集型任务（GPQA +3.03）提升显著，多模态任务（MMStar +1.53）也有改进。

Conclusion: NGM作为训练无关的即插即用模块，能有效增强大模型的知识检索能力，且无需额外训练。

Abstract: Recent studies introduce conditional memory modules that decouple knowledge storage from neural computation, enabling more direct knowledge access. Compared to MoE, which relies on dynamic computation paths, explicit lookup provides a more efficient knowledge retrieval mechanism. However, these approaches still depend on learned memory embeddings, requiring additional training and limiting flexibility. To address this, we propose N-gram Memory (NGM), a training-free, plug-and-play module composed of a Causal N-Gram Encoder and a Cosine-Gated Memory Injector. The Causal N-Gram Encoder directly averages the pretrained token embeddings of the backbone model to construct N-gram representations, thereby eliminating the need to train separate N-gram embeddings from scratch. This design requires neither an additional memory table nor a retrieval pipeline. The Cosine-Gated Memory Injector then uses a non-parametric cosine gate with ReLU to modulate the retrieved embeddings into the contextual representations. We evaluate NGM on the Qwen3 series from 0.6B to 14B across eight benchmarks. NGM improves average performance by 0.5 to 1.2 points, with particularly clear gains on code generation and knowledge-intensive tasks (e.g., +3.0 on LiveCodeBench and +3.03 on GPQA for Qwen3-14B). Moreover, NGM also improves performance in multimodal benchmarks (e.g., MMStar +1.53 on Qwen3-VL-2B).

</details>


### [128] [TOBench: A Task-Oriented Omni-Modal Benchmark for Real-World Tool-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16909)
*Zhiqiang Liu,Wenhui Dong,Yilang Tan,Yuwen Qu,Haochen Yin,Chenyang Si*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了MM-ToolBench，一个面向全能模态工具使用的基准测试和评估框架，包含100个可执行任务、27个MCP服务器和324个工具，采用闭环多模态验证机制，评估结果显示最强模型Claude Opus 4.6仅达32.0%任务成功率，远低于人类基准94.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准通常孤立评估工具使用、计算机使用和多模态推理，缺乏对端到端全能模态工具使用的评估，与现实工作流存在差距。

Method: 构建了MM-ToolBench基准，包含客户服务和智能创作两大任务族（20个子类别），基于MCP协议集成27个服务器和324个工具；设计闭环多模态验证机制，要求代理执行工具、检查中间产物并自我修正；采用半自动化构建流水线（场景发现、任务实例化、评估器合成、人工审计）。

Result: 在15个当代代理模型上评估，Claude Opus 4.6仅达32.0%成功率，人类基准为94.0%，表明该基准具有高挑战性。

Conclusion: MM-ToolBench可作为评估和推进下一代全能模态工具使用代理的实用基础，通过闭环多模态验证弥合现实与基准的差距。

Abstract: Tool-using agents are increasingly expected to operate across realistic professional workflows, where they must interpret multimodal inputs, coordinate external tools, inspect intermediate artifacts, and revise their actions before producing a final result. Existing benchmarks, however, often evaluate tool use, computer use, and multimodal reasoning in isolation, leaving a gap between benchmark settings and end-to-end omni-modal tool use in the real world. To address this gap, we introduce MM-ToolBench, a benchmark and evaluation harness for task-oriented omni-modal tool use. MM-ToolBench contains 100 executable tasks from two macro task families, Customer Service and Intelligent Creation, covering 20 subcategory slices and supported by 27 MCP servers with 324 tools. The central design of MM-ToolBench is closed-loop multimodal verification: agents must execute tools, inspect rendered or transformed artifacts, and self-correct when outputs fail task-specific requirements. To make such evaluation scalable and verifiable, MM-ToolBench couples MCP-based execution with task-specific grounded evaluators and a semi-automated construction pipeline for scenario discovery, task instantiation, evaluator synthesis, and human audit. Experiments on 15 contemporary agentic models show that MM-ToolBench remains highly challenging: Claude Opus 4.6, commonly regarded as one of the strongest coding-agent models, achieves only 32.0% task success, far below the 94.0% human benchmark. We envision MM-ToolBench as a practical foundation for evaluating and advancing next-generation omni-modal tool-using agents through closed-loop multimodal verification.

</details>


### [129] [Reliability and Effectiveness of Autonomous AI Agents in Supply Chain Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17036)
*Carol Xuan Long,David Simchi-Levi,Feng Zhu,Huangyuan Su,Andre P. Calmon,Flavio P. Calmon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究在多层供应链（MIT啤酒游戏）中使用自主生成式AI代理，发现推理时杠杆（模型选择、策略护栏、数据共享、提示工程）影响性能。推理模型超越人类，优化后成本降低67%，但引入"代理牛鞭效应"（决策不可靠性跨层级放大）。提出基于GRPO的后训练框架提升可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索自主AI代理在复杂供应链管理中的表现，识别关键性能杠杆，并解决多代理系统中决策可靠性不足的问题。

Method: 通过MIT啤酒游戏进行实验，测试四种推理时杠杆；提出数学框架刻画代理牛鞭效应；采用GRPO强化学习后训练共享基础LLM，使用系统级供应链奖励进行优化。

Result: 推理模型（如o1）在库存管理和成本控制上超越人类；优化后成本降低67%；存在显著的代理牛鞭效应（跨设施和时间维度的决策方差放大）；GRPO后训练减少尾事件并提升可靠性。

Conclusion: 自主AI代理在供应链中表现优异但可靠性不足，GRPO后训练能有效缓解代理牛鞭效应，提升多代理系统的稳健性。

Abstract: This paper studies autonomous generative AI agents in multi-echelon supply chains using the MIT Beer Game. We identify four inference-time levers that shape performance: model selection, policies and guardrails, centralized data sharing, and prompt engineering. Model capability is the dominant factor: an out-of-the-box reasoning model exceeds human-level performance, and optimized reasoning models reduce costs by up to 67% relative to human teams. However, strong average performance masks substantial reliability risks. We introduce the agent bullwhip effect, the amplification of decision unreliability across echelons, manifesting along two dimensions: decision variance increases both across facilities at the same point in time and within the same facility across time. We develop a mathematical framework showing that this phenomenon is inherent to multi-agent systems that involve coordination and information delays, and we demonstrate that repeated sampling fails to meaningfully reduce it. To address this limitation, we propose a Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)-based reinforcement-learning post-training framework that trains a shared base LLM using system-level supply-chain rewards. GRPO post-training substantially reduces tail events, curtails agent bullwhip, and improves the reliability of autonomous supply-chain agents.

</details>


### [130] [MADP: A Multi-Agent Pipeline for Sustainable Document Processing with Human-in-the-Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17159)
*Diego Gosmar,Giovanni Zenezini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MADP是一个多智能体文档处理自动化架构，结合深度学习分类解析与大语言模型提取，通过人类验证保持准确性。在10万张发票/年的场景下可减少约70%人力，实际部署97%自动化率，98.5%文档级准确率，并显著降低碳排放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业文档处理依赖人工，效率低且易出错，亟需自动化方案来降低人力成本和提升准确性。

Method: 提出MADP多智能体架构，包含五个专用智能体（分类、分割、解析、提取、验证），结合Human-in-the-Loop机制和Prompt Fine Tuning with Feedback Inheritance (PFTFI)方法。

Result: 年处理10万张发票场景下FTE需求减少约70%；955份真实文档部署实现97.0%全流程自动化率；100份文档分层评估达到98.5%准确率；碳排放、能耗、用水分别降低69%、69%、63%。

Conclusion: 混合AI+人工验证的MADP架构能够高效自动化企业文档处理，大幅降低成本和环境影响，多种LLM后端的对比为生产部署提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Document processing automation remains a critical challenge in enterprise environments, where traditional manual approaches are labor-intensive and error-prone. We present MADP, a multi-agent architecture that addresses the challenge of automating document processing in enterprise settings by combining deep learning-based classification and parsing with large language model extraction, while maintaining accuracy through selective human validation. Our system integrates five specialized agents--Classificator, Splitter, Parser, Extraction, and Validator--with a Human-in-the-Loop (HITL) mechanism and a novel Prompt Fine Tuning with Feedback Inheritance (PFTFI) approach. The operational analysis on a production use-case scenario of 100,000 invoices per year indicates a potential reduction of Full-Time Equivalent (FTE) requirements by approximately 70%. Production deployment on 955 real-world documents processed through January 2026 achieves a 97.0% full-pipeline automation rate, with only 3% requiring non-AI fallback. Ablation evaluation on a stratified 100-document subset (5 documents per each of 20 supplier/document-type categories) demonstrates that the full MADP configuration with Human-in-the-Loop supervision attains 98.5% document-level accuracy. Additionally, we present a comprehensive sustainability analysis showing that our hybrid AI+HITL approach reduces CO2 emissions by 69%, energy consumption by 69%, and water usage by 63% compared to traditional manual processing. Benchmark comparisons of multiple LLM backends (Granite-Docling, Mistral-Small, DeepSeek-OCR) provide practical insights for deployment in production environments.

</details>


### [131] [CatalyticMLLM: A Graph-Text Multimodal Large Language Model for Catalytic Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17254)
*Yanjie Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出QE-Catalytic-V2，一个统一的图-文本多模态大语言模型，用于催化材料的性质预测和逆设计，将两个任务整合在同一模型和共享表示空间中，形成闭环优化流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统催化材料的性质预测和逆结构设计通常被建模为两个独立任务，这种解耦范式会导致生成模型与性质预测模型之间的表示空间和训练目标不一致，引入数据分布偏移和评估器偏差，限制闭环优化的稳定性。

Method: 提出QE-Catalytic-V2，一个统一的图-文本多模态大语言模型，将性质预测和逆设计整合在同一模型和共享表示空间中，利用三维结构和文本信息进行可靠的预测，并生成和筛选物理可行的CIF候选结构。

Result: 实验结果表明，该统一范式在催化松弛能预测和逆设计任务上均优于解耦基线方法，验证了在单一多模态模型中联合建模性质预测和结构生成的有效性。

Conclusion: 统一的多模态大语言模型范式能够有效解决催化材料设计中解耦方法的一致性问题，实现更稳定的闭环优化。

Abstract: Property prediction and inverse structural design of catalytic materials are typically modeled as two independent tasks: the former predicts target properties from given structures, whereas the latter generates candidate structures according to desired properties. Although the decoupled paradigm facilitates the implementation of a ``generation--evaluation--screening'' workflow, the inconsistency between the generative model and the property prediction model in terms of representation spaces and training objectives can readily introduce data distribution shifts and evaluator bias, thereby limiting the stability of closed-loop optimization.
  In this work, we propose QE-Catalytic-V2, a unified graph--text multimodal large language model for catalytic materials, which integrates property prediction and inverse design within the same model and shared representation space. Under this unified framework, QE-Catalytic-V2 can not only perform reliable property prediction by leveraging three-dimensional structures and textual information, but also generate and screen physically feasible CIF candidates conditioned on target properties, thereby forming a closed-loop optimization workflow of ``inverse design--prediction--screening--redesign.'' Experimental results demonstrate that this unified paradigm outperforms decoupled baselines on both catalytic relaxed-energy prediction and inverse design tasks, validating the effectiveness of jointly modeling property prediction and structure generation within a single multimodal model.

</details>


### [132] [MetaCogAgent: A Metacognitive Multi-Agent LLM Framework with Self-Aware Task Delegation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17292)
*Chenyu Wang,Yang Shu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MetaCogAgent，一个为每个LLM智能体配备元认知自我评估单元的多智能体框架，通过任务-能力对齐评估、自适应委派协议和能力边界学习，在减少API调用的情况下提升任务准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有框架基于预定义角色分配任务，忽略了智能体能否准确评估自身能力边界的问题，导致其过度自信地执行超出自身专长的任务。

Method: 为每个智能体配备元认知自我评估单元，包含三类模块：(1) 结合口头不确定性与历史能力概况的自评估机制；(2) 将低置信度任务路由到更合适智能体的自适应委派协议；(3) 通过控制论反馈迭代优化智能体能力模型的能力边界学习模块。

Result: 在MetaCog-Eval基准上（700任务、5个认知维度）达到82.4%任务准确率，比最优路由基线高8.7%，API调用比AutoGen少5%、比集成投票少34%。

Conclusion: 元认知组件对系统整体性能有显著贡献，通过自我评估和自适应委派可有效避免智能体在超出自身能力范围的任务上过度自信地执行。

Abstract: Multi-agent large language model (LLM) systems have shown promise for solving complex tasks through agent collaboration. However, existing frameworks assign tasks based on predefined roles without considering whether an agent can accurately assess its own competence boundaries, leading to overconfident execution on tasks beyond its expertise. Inspired by metacognition theory from cognitive science, we propose MetaCogAgent, a multi-agent LLM framework where each agent is equipped with a Metacognitive Self-Assessment Unit that evaluates task-capability alignment before execution. The framework introduces three contributions: (1) a self-assessment mechanism that estimates per-task confidence by combining verbalized uncertainty with historical capability profiles; (2) an adaptive delegation protocol that routes low-confidence tasks to better-suited agents through cross-agent evaluation; and (3) a capability boundary learning module that iteratively refines each agent's competence model via cybernetic feedback. Experiments on our constructed MetaCog-Eval benchmark (700 tasks across 5 cognitive dimensions) demonstrate that MetaCogAgent achieves 82.4% task accuracy -- 8.7% above the best routing baseline -- while using 5% fewer API calls than AutoGen and 34% fewer than ensemble voting. Ablation studies confirm that each metacognitive component contributes to overall system performance.

</details>


### [133] [ADR: An Agentic Detection System for Enterprise Agentic AI Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17380)
*Chenning Li,Pan Hu,Justin Xu,Baris Ozbas,Olivia Liu,Caroline Van,Manxue Li,Wei Zhou,Mohammad Alizadeh,Pengyu Zhang,KK Sriramadhesikan,Ming Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出首个大规模企业级AI Agent安全防护框架ADR，包含传感器、探索器和检测器三个组件，在Uber部署超10个月，覆盖7200+主机，每日处理超10000次Agent会话，在ADR-Bench上实现零误报检测67%攻击，F1分数超越基线方法2-4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端点检测与响应(EDR)工具无法观测Agent推理过程、提示词及因果链；静态规则防御泛化能力差；LLM推理检测成本过高。亟需一套专为AI Agent设计的可规模化、高鲁棒性的安全检测框架。

Method: ADR系统包含三个组件：(1) ADR Sensor——采集高保真Agent遥测数据；(2) ADR Explorer——系统化预部署红队测试与困难样例生成；(3) ADR Detector——双层级在线检测，结合快速分类与上下文感知推理实现可扩展检测。通过MCP协议对Agent操作进行全链路监控。

Result: 在Uber生产环境部署10个月，覆盖7200+主机，每日处理10000+Agent会话，检测到26类数百个凭证暴露，精确率97.2%。在ADR-Bench(302任务,17种攻击技术,133个MCP服务器)上零误报检测67%攻击，F1分数超越ALRPHFS、GuardAgent、LlamaFirewall基线2-4倍。在AgentDojo上检测全部攻击，93任务中仅3个误报。

Conclusion: ADR系统证明了大规模AI Agent安全防护的可行性，通过专用遥测+双层级检测架构实现了高精度、低成本的生产级防护，为AI Agent安全领域提供了有效的框架和基准测试集。

Abstract: We present the Agentic AI Detection and Response (ADR) system, the first large-scale, production-proven enterprise framework for securing AI agents operating through the Model Context Protocol (MCP). We identify three persistent challenges in this domain: (1) limited observability -- existing Endpoint Detection and Response (EDR) tools see file writes but not the agent reasoning, prompts, or causal chains linking intent to execution; (2) insufficient robustness -- static defenses constrained by pre-defined rules fail to generalize across diverse attack techniques and enterprise contexts; and (3) high detection costs -- LLM-based inference is prohibitively expensive at scale. ADR addresses these challenges via three components: the ADR Sensor for high-fidelity agentic telemetry, the ADR Explorer for systematic pre-deployment red teaming and hard-example generation, and the ADR Detector for scalable, two-tier online detection combining fast triage with context-aware reasoning. Deployed at Uber for over ten months, ADR has sustained reliable detection in production with growing adoption reaching over 7,200 unique hosts and processing over 10,000 agent sessions daily, uncovering hundreds of credential exposures across 26 categories and enabling a shift-left prevention layer (97.2% precision, 206 detected credentials). To validate the approach and enable community adoption, we introduce ADR-Bench (302 tasks, 17 techniques, 133 MCP servers), where ADR achieves zero false positives while detecting 67% of attacks -- outperforming three state-of-the-art baselines (ALRPHFS, GuardAgent, LlamaFirewall) by 2--4x in F1-score. On AgentDojo (public prompt injection benchmark), ADR detects all attacks with only three false alarms out of 93 tasks.

</details>


### [134] [Computational Challenges in Token Economics: Bridging Economic Theory and AI System Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17410)
*Ou Wu,Yingjun Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"计算代币经济学"概念和"代币经济学三难困境"，分析了在大语言模型实时推理系统中实施代币经济原则时面临的计算挑战，包括细粒度估值、低延迟执行和分配最优性之间的根本矛盾，并划分了实时价值核算、约束资源分配和经济感知系统架构三个主要技术挑战领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管代币经济学已成为理解大语言模型系统中资源分配和价值创造的重要视角，但高阶经济理论与现代AI基础设施的计算现实之间存在显著差距，亟需弥合。
